**What it did:** a text that is a fixed word in full (`shadow`, a tier or IV word) brings no guesses in `/want`; spawn reports are rows of `SpawnReport` (migration 010) and are swept at start and hourly; `/gym show` shows one address line; the lines of a train and the start-time DM do not name an anonymous maker; `defer: 'late'` for `/settings`, the wants, the gym targets, `/friend-code`, `/nickname`, `/iam`, `/iamnot` and `/wild`; `/admin setup-forums` closes a forum to posts by members and names forums by `settings.regionForumSuffix` (Matt, 2026-10-13: `-meetups`, a config setting; per server in the database only if a server asks).

**Staging (short; one migration, no `deploy-commands`):**

- [ ] Speed: `/settings`, `/want`, `/target`, `/nickname`, `/wild` answer in about half the time (the log's `ms`, no `deferMs`); `/iam` with a role that needs a request still answers.
- [ ] `/want shadow`: no Meadow Pattern.
- [ ] `/wild`, then kill the bot (close the window), start it: the report goes at start if overdue, else at its time. The reports left from 2026-10-13 are not in the table and stay until deleted by hand.
- [ ] `/gym refresh` then `/gym show`: one address line as Google writes it.
- [ ] A train made with privacy Anonymous and auto-status None: add a stop, set the start; the post and the second account's DM name nobody.
- [ ] `/admin setup-forums`: the test forum is renamed `test-region-meetups` and "closed to posts by members"; the second account cannot make a post there and can still write in one; the bot still makes posts. Run again: "is ready".

**Left open:**

- (Done 2026-10-13, on Matt's word: the lines about a raid and its meeting time no longer name a reporter who asked not to be named.)
- A late acknowledgement that reached Discord while its answer was lost leaves "thinking…" (the direct answer is then refused as a second acknowledgement). Rare; `answer` could fall back to `editReply` on that refusal.
- A machine clock a second ahead of Discord's makes every late command defer at once (the old behaviour); `lateMs` in the debug line shows it.
- A spawn report whose process is killed between the send and the insert stays (the message id is only known after the send).
- The legacy bot's gym detail view prints the new `formatted_address` part as one more line (cosmetic, until cutover).
- `reservePosts` denies Send Messages only on overwrites of the forum itself; a role that gets Administrator can always post.

**For 5B (Matt, 2026-10-13):** `/admin setup-forums` only adds: a forum whose region is gone stays. `/region remove` must say what becomes of the forum of the region (suggested: end its open parties, then ask whether to delete the forum or leave it locked as history). Forums of regions deleted outside the bot can be found as forums that party rows name and no region does; `/admin setup-forums` could list them.

**Future enhancement (Matt, 2026-10-13): trains that loop or turn back** (built in plan 8B-3, 2026-10-15: see "From Phase 8B-3"). Raid hours and raid days turn raids over every half hour, so a train is short and its route is often run again, as a loop or in reverse. Suggested shape: one setting of the train, "at the end of the route": finish (as now), loop (after the last stop the first), turn back (the route in reverse from the end); loop and turn back exclude each other by construction; a train set to finish gets a "Reverse" button in the move panel at either end.

**Phase 8 (the admin migration guide):** one `/admin setup-forums` per server makes the forums (and may be run again at any time); the bot needs Manage Channels and Manage Permissions for it; `regionForumSuffix` in the settings; forums count one channel per region against the server's 500, posts count as threads (1,000 active per server), which ends the legacy bot's pressure on the channel limit.

**Phase 8, at the end of the migration (Matt, 2026-10-13): go through `data/settings.json` and remove what is obsolete.** Every key the new bot never reads (what only the legacy bot used: the updater intervals, legacy channel and emoji names and the like) leaves the file, the schema (`src/config/settings.ts`), the test fixture and `withoutRetiredSettings` gets the removed keys so that an old file still starts; the migration guide lists what went. Do it once the legacy code is removed, by searching the code for each key.

**The staging of this round ran on 2026-10-13** (both accounts; logs `var/stage/logs/bot-8.log` and `bot-9.log`, no warning or error): migration 010 at start; `/wants`, `/settings show`, `/targets` in 216 to 272 ms without a deferral, `/wild` in 475 ms; `/want shadow` without Meadow Pattern; a spawn report made overdue in the test database was deleted at the start after a kill; `/gym refresh` shows one address line; an anonymous train and an anonymous raid name nobody in the post, the lines or the start-time DM (Tester's); `/admin setup-forums` renamed both forums to `-meetups` and closed them (Tester's New Post button is disabled, writing in a post works, the bot still makes posts), and a second run says only "is ready". Nothing new was found. Not tried: `/iam` with a role that needs a request, and a report that is deleted at its own time after a restart (only the overdue case).

**Needs a design (Matt, 2026-10-13): an audit trail for staff.** Anonymous means anonymous in the posts only: moderators must be able to find out who reported a raid or made a train and who changed what (boss, times, gym, route). Today the party row has the maker (`createdById`) and the log has some changes, but nothing a moderator can ask the bot. To be designed: what is recorded (a table of changes per party, with who, what and when, kept how long after the party ended) and how staff read it (a staff command in the post, for the member alone; perhaps a search by member). Candidate for 5C (member settings for staff) or a plan of its own.

## From Phase 5B (regions, 2026-10-14)

Built overnight on Matt's word as `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-phase-5b-regions.md` says (its ten decisions are at its top): a design brief, an Opus prototype with two fix rounds after two reviews and a re-review, one Sonnet transcription (identical to the prototype), an Opus whole-branch review and one fix wave (1baa6df..919ab50). 4,369 unit and 287 database tests on the tip. Nothing has touched Discord; the staging checklist is at the end of the plan (`deploy-commands -- --guild` first: `/region` and `/bounds` are new; migration 011).

**For Matt (rulings made overnight, easy to change):** an import creates at most 25 channels at a time (a big first import would otherwise outlive Discord's 15-minute answer; importing the same file again does the rest); creating channels and deleting a forum through `/region` need the staff member's own Manage Channels (as the self-roles need Manage Roles); an orphan forum named `<channel>-meetups` in the channel's category is taken back by a new import of that channel instead of a second forum being made.

**Left open:**

- How Discord rewrites the names of created channels and which error it gives for a full category (guessed from the message); the cache checks of the real gateway (`channelKind`, a deleted category) are proved only by staging. A news channel named for an announcement is not found (text channels only).
- The forum an import makes through `setupForums` is made also for staff without Manage Channels and has no audit reason; `/admin setup-forums` still makes a second forum beside a kept orphan (only the import takes one back).
- A question whose service call throws something other than a `UserError` after it was marked answered stays dead (later presses only acknowledged; an "Importing…" message keeps saying so), as in `/gym`.
- An export sends ten files per message without a byte cap per message (a file is at most about 1 MB under the corner limit).
- A removal holds the admin lock while it ends the region's parties one by one, and an import while it creates channels and forums: gym administration waits meanwhile.
- `/region list` counts gyms in no region of any server across all servers (fine for one server; production has 411).
- A database where two rows share a channel gets no unique key (a warning at every start says how to add it by hand); the oldest row counts everywhere.
- The adoption of an orphan forum matches an area to its imported region by the area's name.

**Phase 8 (cutover):** the legacy bot builds its static-map address from every point of an outline, so an outline of thousands of corners written by the new bot breaks the legacy region picture: never import traced outlines into a database the legacy bot still uses. The bot needs Manage Channels for an import that creates channels. Staff must know that `/region` imports from Google My Maps as KML (not KMZ).

## From Phase 5C (raid boss administration, nicknames, member settings, the export, 2026-10-14)

Built overnight as `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-phase-5c-boss-admin.md` says (its decisions at its top are the controller's rulings; Matt has not seen them): brief, Opus prototype, two reviewers, a re-review, two fix rounds, a rebase onto the finished 5B, one Sonnet transcription, an Opus whole-branch review and one fix wave. 4,498 unit and 294 database tests on the tip. This finishes the building of Phase 5. Nothing has touched Discord; the staging checklist is at the end of the plan (`deploy-commands -- --guild` first).

**Left open:**

- The out-of-date warning is said again after every restart while the feed stays down (nothing stores that it was said).

- Announcements go to text channels only: a news channel named `bot-updates` is not found (staging note).
- `/admin boss sync` does not refresh the game data.
- The staff audit trail is not built (needs a design with Matt).

**Phase 8 (cutover):** run `npm run role-auth:export` against production before the move and recreate what it lists as native command permissions (Server Settings, Integrations); it maps every legacy command name.

## From Phase 6B (`/counters`, `/find-region`, 2026-10-14)

Built overnight as `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-phase-6b-counters-find-region.md` says (its decisions at its top are the controller's rulings): Opus prototype, two reviewers, a re-review, two fix rounds, a rebase onto the finished 6A, one Sonnet transcription, an Opus whole-branch review and its fix wave. 4,735 unit and 294 database tests on the tip. This finishes the building of Phase 6. Nothing has touched Discord, Pokebattler or Nominatim; the staging checklist is at the end of the plan.

**Left open:**

- Pokebattler's names for shadow regional forms (`ARCANINE_HISUIAN_SHADOW_FORM`) and whether it answers 400 or 404 for an unknown boss or Pokébox, and what an empty Pokébox gives: staging.
- The guard of `/counters` reads the plan (and a Pokébox id) before the deferral, and `execute` reads it again.
- A Google Maps link that carries only a place name (`/maps/place/<name>`) is refused by `/find-region` (it could search the name).

## From the staging run of 5B, 5C, 6A and 6B (2026-10-14, both accounts)

Run on the test server with the checklists at the end of the four plans (commands registered for the test server, then globally for the DM tests; migration 011 applied at start; KML uploaded by script in the browser). Everything tried passed except what is listed under "To fix". The test server was left as it was (the channel and forum the import made were deleted by the removal test, the outlines restored). A test issue was filed in `GITHUB_REPO` (Googer/PgP-Data#599; that repository had been archived, which GitHub answers with 403, and Matt unarchived it).

**Passed:** `/region import` (preview, creating a channel and a forum, "did you mean", the KMZ sentence, `channel:`, an unchanged re-import, taking back an orphan forum, without Manage Channels: the new area left out with the reason), `/region export`, `/region list` (also "its channel is gone" and forums whose region is gone), `/region remove` (the question counts the parties, the post says why, keep or delete the forum), `/admin setup-forums` naming orphan forums, `/bounds` in a channel, a post and elsewhere, as staff and as a member, `/region` not offered to a member; `/admin boss list|add|hide|autoset|shiny|remove|sync` (an autoset egg hatched as the boss, the shiny mark in the post, the throttle of three a minute counted from the first use), the announcement in `bot-updates` (one message, no ping), nicknames with both refusals, `/help command:admin` and `admin boss`, `/admin member-settings`, `npm run role-auth:export`; `/rank` (both caps, one column when they agree, Eevee's line in Master League, outside the PvP category refused, in a DM), `/boss-tier` in its three forms, `/maps`, `/find-gym` by `#n`, digits and a number no gym has, `/request` in a raid's post (title, gym-number label, `@`, `&`, `#` and `github.com/` broken); `/counters` (public in a post with the post's boss and tier, a typed boss with the post's tier, refused in an egg's post, Shadow T5 of a default-form boss, Mega, the Pokébox, in a DM; about 1.2 seconds), `/settings pokebattler-id`, `/find-region` with coordinates, a maps link, a town and a place far away.

**To fix (one small round):**

- `/region import`'s answer after Import says "new outline" for an outline the preview called unchanged (`importedLine` in `src/commands/regions/import.ts` has no `unchanged`).
- `/raid`'s boss autocomplete (`bossChoices`, `raid-input.ts`) matches the bosses' names only, never nicknames: "sparky" offers nothing, though sending it works (`reportedBoss` asks `ctx.pokemon.exact`).
- `/find-region` searches Nominatim strictly inside the box of the regions (`bounded=1`), so a town just outside it (Bentleyville, 40.117 N against an edge at 40.1) is "no place called … in or near this server's regions": search a margin around the box, and say that a place found lies in no region.
- `/find-region` chose "East Fredericktown Road" (a line) over the town of Fredericktown (a point) as "the largest of 3 places": a settlement or boundary should win over a road whatever their sizes.
- `/rank` refuses megas and primals; since this season some Go Battle League cups allow megas (Matt, 2026-10-14), so rank them.

**Cosmetic:** a forum the import creates lands among the text channels of its category, not beside the other forums; `/counters` names counters as Pokebattler does ("Kyurem White Form") beside the catalogue's names ("Zacian (Crowned Sword)", plain "Landorus" for the Incarnate Forme): name them all by the catalogue; `/boss-tier` writes "boss of T5 ✨ raids now. ✨ can be shiny."; `/admin member-settings` does not show the Pokebattler id; `/request` in the post of a raid that ended says to use it in a raid's post (say it ended); a GitHub refusal is logged with its status only (log GitHub's message too: "archived" would have said it at once).

**Left open:** shadow regional forms on Pokebattler and an empty or private Pokébox (no such boss or box to try); the test server's own registration was removed after the run (Matt, 2026-10-14), so the dev application has only the global one, as production will: every command once, also in DMs; register with `npm run deploy-commands -- --global` from now on (a global change may take a client reload to show).

## From the fix round after the staging of 5B to 6B (2026-10-14)

Done by the controller without a plan document (each fix with its failing test first), then one Opus review of the diff and its fixes; full check (4,748 unit tests) and build on the tip, the database suite (294) before the review fixes, which touch no database code. Gantt rows 6S (the staging run) and 6F (this round). Nothing has touched Discord; no migration, and no command definition changed (the global registration is current).

**Built:**

- `/region import`: the answer after Import says "outline unchanged" for an outline that stayed as it was.
- `/raid`'s boss list offers the boss a nickname (or its beginning) names, after the bosses whose name fits.
- `/find-region` searches the regions' box widened by 10 km (`SEARCH_MARGIN_KM`), so a town just outside is found and said to lie in none of the regions; of several places by one name a settlement or boundary wins, then anything but a road, then the largest (`bestPlace`); the answer says "the most likely of N places".
- `/rank` ranks megas and primals and offers them (some cups of Go Battle League allow megas, Matt, 2026-10-14).
- `/boss-tier`: "is a boss of T5 raids now, and can be shiny." when every tier can be; otherwise the marks with the legend on a line of its own.
- `/request` in the post of a party that ended says so (not in the summary post); GitHub's answers are read with a 16 KB limit, and a refusal is logged with a word for its kind (`archived`, `bad-credentials`, `not-found`, …).
- `/counters` names Pokebattler's ids with or without `_FORM` and bare species ids (`LANDORUS`) by the catalogue.
- A new forum is moved after the last forum of its category.
- `/admin member-settings` did show the Pokebattler id already (it was not set yet when the staging looked); a test pins it.

**To look at on Discord:** an import that creates a forum in a category with forums (where it lands); `/boss-tier` of a shiny boss; `/raid` with a boss's nickname; `/find-region Bentleyville` and `Fredericktown`; `/rank` of a mega; `/counters` against a boss whose counters include White Kyurem or Landorus; `/request` in an ended post and in the summary post.

**Left open (from the review):** a nickname together with a tier or "shadow" word (`genie t5`) offers nothing in `/raid`'s boss list (the search wants every word); a bare Pokebattler id whose default form has two words (`ZYGARDE` for `ZYGARDE_FIFTY_PERCENT_FORM`) is still shown title-cased.

## From Phase 7A (the screenshot reader, 2026-10-14)

Built without Discord; on the 28 samples: clock, countdown, egg or hatched, heads and shadow 28/28, CP 9/9, gym 28/28 through Pine's search (the raw text 25/28), about 1.0 to 1.7 s a screenshot and 640 to 680 MB at the reading process's peak on this laptop. For 7B:

- ~~**Wiring:**~~ Done in 7B. call `OcrAssets.ensure()` at start when screenshots are read (the first read otherwise waits for about 15 MB of downloads), and `OcrService.close()` at shutdown. The attachment download caps at `MAX_SCREENSHOT_BYTES` (`src/services/ocr-service.ts`); memory is that times `settings.ocr.queue` at worst.
- ~~**The rule of doubts:**~~ Done in 7B (Matt: report with what is known; refuse only no raid screen or no gym; ask only the gym). which doubts let a raid be made without asking. To be designed with the 7B plan; a starting point: make a raid unasked only from a raid screen with a clear gym and no doubt but `time-guessed` (said in the post), and ask about everything else.
- ~~**The Android file name as a hint of the time**~~ Done in 7B (`Screenshot_20261014-132301.jpg`): against a screenshot reposted a day later within 15 minutes of the same clock time.
- ~~**Purple on the name-alone path:**~~ Done in 7B. when a plain and a shadow boss of one species are both listed and the CP was not read, the names tie and the boss stays unknown; the purple could decide (final review, deferred: the member is asked anyway).
- **A speed unit split twice** ("1.25 K /s") may still read as a dotted clock when no h:mm clock was read; not seen on the samples.
- **Shadow T3 stamina** 4,000 is confirmed by a shadow Kirlia raid (CP 8554; the screenshot is in `#test-region` of the test server for the staging).

For Phase 9: the Linux check of the threads' priority (the test runs on Linux only), `ONNXRUNTIME_NODE_INSTALL=skip` for a CPU image or the CUDA 12 provider for a GPU image, a glibc base image, `.env` not readable by the user that runs the bot (the reading process gets none of it, but parses members' files), and a memory limit for the reading process.

- **Expect** many review copies: every screenshot the reader had a doubt about goes there (Matt: fine), within `ocr.reviewPerDay` (50) per server and `ocr.keepMegabytes` (4096).
- **Docker Desktop's IPv6 forwarding** broke on this laptop on 2026-10-14 (see CLAUDE.md's gotchas); the database suite runs with `TESTCONTAINERS_HOST_OVERRIDE=127.0.0.1` until Docker Desktop is restarted.

## From the staging run of 7B (2026-10-14, both accounts)

Every step of the plan's checklist passed, passive reading included (`ocr.passive` on in `var/stage/settings.json`, the Message Content intent switched on for the dev application): `/read-screenshot` (the shadow Kirlia read right in every element, its gym a guess) with both kinds of feedback; `/raid screenshot:` reported unasked, the post's "Screenshot feedback" for the reporter and for the second account (stored as `other`); the guessed time's line kept by `/duration`, taken away by `/hatch`; a typed gym with two hits asked about, an exact name taken as clear; a mention with two screenshots (🤔, two raids, the message deleted), one with words and an existing raid (kept, ✅, the post linked); the gym question refused to the second account and gone after two minutes; an ordinary picture answered once with a mention and not at all without; the T1, T5 and mega egg pictures; `/gym duplicates` and the warning of `/gym edit` (letters only: "Temple-Sinai" is "Temple Sinai"); hard-linked review copies, no names in the records, no read text in the log. Samples were attached in the built-in browser from a message in `#the-bot-lab` (fetched from Discord's CDN into a `DataTransfer`; local files cannot be fetched or framed from discord.com). The test server now has five gyms of the samples (#13 to #17: Temple Sinai, Temple Sinai Garden, Congregation Poale Zedeck, Catahecassa Blackhoof - War Chief of the Shawnee, Gregory Rudzki Memorial Courts).

To fix (one small round):

- **"I found no clock"** is said for `time-guessed` also when a clock was read but did not fit the upload (a screenshot taken hours before): say which of the two it was ("the phone's clock (1:38) does not fit when it was posted"). Matt's feedback in the review folder says the same.
- **Passive reading deletes like a mention** (Matt, 2026-10-14; the legacy bot deleted a message once any image of it made a raid, `app/process-image.js`): a message read passively is deleted when every image made or joined a raid and it says nothing else, otherwise marked ✅ as now; pictures that are no raid screen stay untouched and unanswered.
- **Cosmetic:** the private answer to a pick in the mention's gym question replies to the question it has just deleted, so Discord shows "Message could not be loaded" above it.

Seen, deliberate: a gym name read off a screen that is exactly one gym's name is still asked about when a longer name begins with it ("Temple Sinai" beside "Temple Sinai Garden"), since the game cuts long names; a typed name is not. A screenshot with feedback "Something is wrong" by anybody gets no `label`, also when another member says "All right" later.

a Pokébox answer is private wherever it is asked (also in the bot's DM), with nicknames and CP, "Your Pokébox on Pokebattler" (no user number). `/rank` with rank and percentage beside the picture, the caps stacked, league buttons (the shown one greyed), "Evolution line" / "Hide evolution line", pvpivs.com. `/help` as an overview of names per group with a menu of groups, a group's page with descriptions and a menu of its commands, a command's page with usage, examples and back buttons; ## From Phase 8B-2 (`/counters` with its dialog, `/rank`, `/help` as components-v2 messages, 2026-10-15)

Built on Matt's go-ahead (plan `2026-10-15-phase-8b2-counters-rank-help.md`, tasks b306925..a261f0a, final fix 39e22bc; prototype reviewed by two Opus reviewers, its fixes by a third; final review: ready to merge, five Minor, all fixed). Check 5,165, build, timer files 10/10; no repository changed (no database suite). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-phase-8b2-staging.md` (no registration, no migration).

- **Rulings made without Matt, to look at in staging:** the dialog's submits count in `/counters`' own window (three a minute in all, for Pokebattler's sake) and a refused submit does not count; opening the dialog and the controls of `/rank` and `/help` count in windows of their own (30 a minute); a message without interaction metadata is nobody's (a press gives a private copy); `/rank` with one cap reads "Up to level 50 · the same rank up to 51"; link buttons without `↗` (Discord draws its own); a type without an emoji is named in words (`Bite (Dark)`); `† a legacy move` only when a shown counter has one; the kept options keep the post's party (an ended party's public answer is never edited) and never the Pokébox number; a Pokébox picked in the dialog takes the checkboxes as shown (the command ticks shadows and megas by itself, the dialog does not); a subcommand inside a group goes back one level (`/admin boss add` → `◀ /admin boss`); `/help` gives groups text room by need.
- **Only staging can show:** whether every client (the phone app above all) shows the checkbox group of a dialog, and what a submit with nothing ticked sends (it is read as nothing ticked either way); a press by the second account on a public answer (a private copy, the answer untouched); an expired token after a restart.
- **Left open:** a dialog submit reads the member's settings twice (guard and handler; two primary-key reads); a control's `as` limit and its command's are equal only by convention (nothing checks it).
- **Components-v2 only (Matt, 2026-10-15):** every message that is more than plain text is a components-v2 message; new code never builds an embed. What still is one goes into 8B-3 beside `/gym show`: the previews and answers of `/gym add|edit|move|delete` (`src/commands/gyms/shared.ts`, `gym-view.ts`), the new-gym and deleted-gym announcements, `/find-gym` (`renderMemberGymView`), `/find-region` and `/bounds` (an embed with a map picture: a v2 message shows an attached file), `/region import`'s preview and answer and `/region remove`. Plain one-line answers stay text. The classic text spawn report (channels without Embed Links) stays until staging shows whether a v2 message needs that permission.

## From Phase 8B-3 (train modes, Reverse, the Move train panel as a components-v2 message, 2026-10-15)

Built on Matt's go-ahead (plan `2026-10-15-phase-8b3-train-modes.md`, tasks 9ea687c..7606164, final fix ff0b7cf; drafts https://claude.ai/artifact/NdZ5QAHbwHagu76w7Fzy1X approved; prototype reviewed by two Opus reviewers, its fixes by a third; final review: ready after fixes, one Important (the staging checklist on migration order) and a Minor in code, both fixed). Check 5,668, build, the database tests of the touched repositories (63), timer files 10/10. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-phase-8b3-staging.md` (`npm run deploy-commands -- --global` first: `/train` has the option `at-end`; migration 013).

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-15):** the mode is set by `/train` and by a menu in the panel; a turned or reversed route is listed in its new order and renumbered; the mode is kept with a saved route (`SavedRoutes.atEnd`); a Loop train may be reversed at any stop, which changes only the order; Previous is off at stop 1 for Finish and Turn back trains; a change of the mode is said in the post without a ping. The other embeds moved from 8B-3 to a plan of their own, 8B-4, after 8W-1 is merged back (it changes `gym-view.ts`, `gym-admin-service.ts` and the `/gym` commands, and adds two embeds of its own: the `/gym import` preview and `gymsAnnouncement`).
- **Rulings made without Matt, to look at in staging:** whoever may arrange the route (signed up, or the train's maker) and staff may set the mode, though the menu is only in the panel, which opens only for who may move the train; a member who may set it but not move the train gets the mode set and the panel closed with the reason. Reverse on a Finish train at stop 1, at the last stop, and on a route finished from its last stop (it starts again there); on a finished Loop train as on a Finish one; never on a Turn back train or a route of fewer than two stops; its button carries the route's first gym, so a second press of one panel is refused. Routes of one stop finish in every mode and say nothing of the mode in the post and `/route show`. A Loop Skip wraps; a Turn back Skip at the second-to-last stop is refused. Removing the current last stop of a loop or turn-back train goes on (to the first stop, or turned back) instead of finishing. The mode menu carries where the train was (a pick on a stale panel is refused). The public "loaded" line leaves out the Finish sentence. Old plain-text panels: a press does its move, the old panel says it was replaced, and the new one follows privately.
- **Only staging can show:** whether Discord would have taken a direct v2 update of an old plain-text panel (not tried); how the closed mode menu reads on a phone ("Loop: back to the first stop").
- **Migration 013 and 8W-1:** 8W-1 holds 012; Kysely refuses a migration that sorts before one already run, and starting the bot migrates. Stage 8B-3 only once 8W-1 is merged; if 013 ran first, delete its row from `kysely_migration` and start again (013 does nothing when the column exists).
- **Left open:** `/route save` and `/route load` of a one-stop route still say the stored mode ("loops"), though such a route finishes; on a Loop train a panel drawn at a gym the train comes round to again accepts a press again (as Previous-then-Next always did).

## From Phase 8W-1 (the Wayfarer export, `/gym import`, `/gym review`, 2026-10-15)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-15-phase-8w1-wayfarer-import.md`, tasks 3a3c5dd..87ee799, each equal to its prototype commit; final review on Opus: ready after fixes, four Important and six Minor; fix wave 4e29c47..212b53a (a stale item no longer blocks the queue, picks by name before picks by place alone (Matt agreed), "None of these" says that the next import adds the listed gyms, a preview counts an item queued only with the same candidates, `missing`/`unknown` only where every cell within 50 m was covered, the custom-id docs), re-reviewed clean; merged with `typescript-migration` in 2af73a6 (which also fixed `round-trips.db.test.ts`, broken since 8B-1 made `/wants` and spawn reports v2). Check 6,000, database suite 323, build green; timer files 10/10. Not yet on Discord or on a full scan: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-phase-8w1-staging.md` (`npm run deploy-commands -- --global` first; migration 012).

- **Rulings to look at in staging:** the decisions listed at the top of the plan ("Made while prototyping"), and from the final review: Skip of a review item runs outside the admin lock (it writes only `queuedAt` of an open item); an item whose gym's Wayfarer id changed under it waits, unshown, for the next import of its server; a gym whose own Wayspot was seen as a stop is `missing` even near an uncovered cell.
- **Only staging can show:** how many review items Pittsburgh's first import makes; how long a first city import holds the admin lock (and so `/gym`); whether Discord takes a File component in a private v2 answer and its later edits. (The export tool's part is kept with the tool.)
- **Left open (Minor, from the final review and its re-review):** two gyms holding one Wayfarer id: the lower id wins whatever the distance, and the other is asked about as `unknown` with a wording that is then wrong; Keep and None of these stop sticking when two files cover different cells (compare only candidates whose cells the new file covered); a pending preview holds whole parsed exports (descriptions and addresses) for 30 minutes; merging several files takes the union of their empty cells, so a cell empty in one file and with a Wayspot in another stays covered (F2's twin across files); `unseen` still looks only at the gym's own cell; `next` says the queue is empty while the counts include waiting items; Back on an item that started waiting says nothing of why. (all closed by 8W-3)
- **Next for this work:** 8W-2 (the Wayspot's picture in posts, after 8B-4); refinements of the userscript after the first real scan.
- The Wayspot export tool's part of this section is kept with the tool.
- **The scanned area in the export, and a bulk delete of gyms outside it** (Matt, 2026-10-15): the export carries the outline of every area the export covers, so that the scanned area is the file's outlines grown by its margin, never the server's regions of the day nor area names (the first Pittsburgh scan named its areas `region-<channel id>`). No format versioning for it: Pine is the only user, the field is simply required (save it again with the new build). The import's preview says where the file's outlines differ from the server's regions and counts the gyms this server may touch that lie in no region of any server and outside the scanned area (listed in the report with their distance); after an applied import its outcome offers "Delete N gyms outside the area…": a second press with the count, some names and what goes with them (saved routes, gym targets), the list worked out again at the press, a gym with an active raid skipped and named, each deleted through `GymAdminService.delete`, one bot-updates post at the end. Offered only for a finished scan (no cells left uncovered in the area) and never with a margin of 0. Pittsburgh at cutover: import, review queue, then this (production's gyms came from a bounding box well past the regions; 411 lie in no region).
- **Google lookups after a mass import go into a slow, lasting queue** (Matt, 2026-10-16): Pittsburgh's first import adds 2,596 gyms and moves some over 25 m, about 5,000 Geocoding and Places requests if done at once. An import asks Google nothing itself: a gym without its address parts or nearby places (or moved since they were fetched) is picked up over time, a bounded number a day (a setting, so the free allowance holds), from the database rather than an in-memory queue, so a restart loses nothing (the hourly upkeep finds them). Search and posts work meanwhile without an address (a gym is saved without one already when there is no key). Today `GymUpkeep`'s slow line is in memory and not bounded per day. Also time a real import against a scratch copy (how long it holds the admin lock); planning alone took 0.55 s on the main thread, writing waits on the database. Google's free usage (since 2025-03-01 per SKU and month instead of the $200 credit): Pine's Places call (`searchNearby` with `displayName,types`) is Nearby Search Pro, 5,000 free a month then $32 per 1,000 (no Essentials tier for Nearby Search); Geocoding and Static Maps are Essentials, 10,000 free each, then $5 and $2. Default limit: 100 gyms a day (about 3,000 Places and 3,000 Geocoding calls a month, room left for `/gym add`, moves and nearest-gym refetches); a higher limit for a catch-up is the operator's call. Pittsburgh's backlog is more than its 2,596 new gyms: every gym whose nearest gym changes has its places fetched again (perhaps 4,000–5,000 Places calls; six to seven weeks at 100 a day). An imported gym takes Wayfarer's address (every Wayspot has one) instead of Geocoding, which stays for gyms added by hand; stops and power spots get no Google lookups at all (Wayfarer's address; no nearby places: 20,000+ Places calls for little). Static map pictures are fetched only when shown and cached by what they show, so imports cost no Static Maps calls.
- **A mass gym import or update is announced in bot updates as one summary** (Matt, 2026-10-15, first asked as a post per change, then, seeing Pittsburgh's numbers, a summary): how many gyms were added, renamed, moved and deleted (by `/gym import`, its bulk delete and `/gym review`), not a list of each (`gymsAnnouncement` of the added gyms gives way to it for an import; a single `/gym add` or `/gym delete` keeps its own post). Components-v2: one container, `GYM_COLOUR`, no pings, through `GymAdminService.announce`. 8B-4 changes only `gymAnnouncement` (one gym added) and its call.
- **The first preview against production** (2026-10-16; the scan above against a scratch copy of the 2026-10-14 dump, migrated; nothing written): 3,213 gyms, 2,957 considered (256 not: in no region and away from the scanned area, the bulk delete's candidates), 2,796 matched (329 by id, 2,338 by name, 129 by place), 2,596 Wayfarer gyms added (every one active; none within 100 m of a gym that may be it), 161 review items (77 match, 16 missing, 68 unknown), 13 Wayfarer gyms just outside the regions, 3,632 Wayspots ignored; 109 moves (median 13 m, at most 49 m), every matched gym gets its picture, 0.55 s. **Of the 292 renames, 123 change only spaces or case**: the spaces are Pine's (126 stored names have outer spaces, from the old gym data; Wayfarer's titles are clean, 2 of 43,057). Such a name is still written (it tidies Pine's data) but is no change (Matt, 2026-10-16): not counted as a rename in the preview, the outcome or the bot-updates summary, and not by itself a reason to write a gym; a name that differs otherwise, also wholly ("Hanna's Town Cabin" → "Klingensmith Cabin"), is a rename. Case only (20, "Monastery fountains" → "Monastery Fountains") is cleanup too (Matt, 2026-10-16). Matches by place within 5 m that change the name entirely ("Hanna's Town Cabin" → "Klingensmith Cabin") are valid and expected (Matt, 2026-10-16): Wayspots get renamed.
- **Search over stops and power spots** (Matt, 2026-10-15; for field tasks, Max Battles and `/wild` near a place, perhaps soon for `/wild`): one index per kind (gyms, stops, power spots), never searched together where the kind is known; every power spot counts as active (Max Battles need an active spot, but the state changes daily and is not scraped). `/wild` merges the three per-kind searches rather than keep a fourth index (scores of separate MiniSearch indexes do not compare): ordered by how well a hit matched (every word exact, a whole word, a beginning, looser, a guess), then the member's region before neighbouring ones, then gym, stop, power spot. Speed is not the concern (MiniSearch, an inverted index; ~43,000 entries) but result quality: about 900 stops a region, so "church", "park", "mural" or "little free library" hit dozens; guesses after real hits and, for stops, only when nothing matched as typed (perhaps one edit at most); common words weigh little; every choice says where it is (street, nearest gym); act on typed text only on a clear hit (rarely, so autocomplete is the way); the `places` field adds noise and needs a look for stops. Before building, index the real export and tune against a list of realistic searches.

## From Phase 8W-1R (the Wayfarer refinements after the first full scan, 2026-10-16)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-16-phase-8w1r-wayfarer-refinements.md`, tasks 2deff37e..ee056bae, each equal to its prototype commit; final review on Opus: ready after fixes, one Important and six Minor; fix wave 4c0d82f8..e37471bc, re-reviewed ready). Check, build and database suite green; timer files 10/10. Migration `2026_10_16_014_gym_lookups` (014 is this plan's: first merge wins). No command registration needed. Not yet on Discord or in Wayfarer: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-8w1r-staging.md` (`npm run migrate` first; save the Pittsburgh export again with the new version so the file has `outlines`).

- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away, 2026-10-16):** the bulk delete is offered only while every current region of the server lies inside the scanned area (the file's outlines grown by its margin): a scan of one new region no longer offers to delete the regionless gyms around all the others (final review F1). It is checked at the preview, at the question and again at the press; a region added, grown or even shrunk since the scan blocks it until a fresh finished scan. One question token per outcome: pressing "Delete N gyms outside the area…" again replaces the question, so an older copy of its buttons answers the newest list (ids still re-checked under the lock). A match that is renamed and moved is written in one row write and counted as renamed only when the move succeeds. The Google line asks the address and the places separately: a gym counts when either reached Google, a failed geocode keeps the gym waiting for its address at the end of the line, a gym gone before the first question is not counted.
- **Rulings from the prototype's review (Matt, 2026-10-16):** bulk-delete candidates only gyms whose nearest region is this server's; a tidy-only name written but not counted (`tidied`).
- **Only staging can show:** how many gyms Pittsburgh's bulk delete offers after the import (the preview against production counted 256 regionless gyms away from the scan); the Google line draining at 100 a day after a city import. (The export tool's part is kept with the tool.)
- **Left open (Minor):** review answers ("Same as", "New gym") still geocode instead of taking Wayfarer's address (`ReviewCandidate` carries no address; a JSON shape change); `VALUES()` in the lookup line's upsert is deprecated since MySQL 8.0.20 (kept until production's version is known); a denied geocode has no backoff, so while Geocoding alone is refused a gym waiting for its address has its places asked (and billed) again every pass, bounded by the daily limit (skip Places and back off on a denied geocode); in the Places-denied path `setAddress(false)` ignores an address request re-queued during the turn (one-line guard `!asking.address`); the review's gathered summary is lost on a crash (documented); no test exercises a `foreign` refusal of the combined rename-and-move writing nothing (region administration takes the same admin lock, so it cannot happen mid-batch). (closed by 8W-3, except the review summary lost on a crash, which stays documented; the export tool's item is kept with the tool)

The legacy `parties/` folder is copied to `C:\Users\<you>\pine-backups\parties-20261016` (member data: never into the repository).
- **9A built:** plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-9a-docker-stack.md`, tasks 3a10779..9b09663, fixes 2fe31a3, 601f0e0, 1b687bf, merged 0f4c03f. `./pine` with `up`, `down`, `restart`, `logs`, `status`, `migrate`, `restore`, `backup`, `deploy-commands`, `update`, `shell`; operator settings in `pine-settings.json`; conventions in `docs/architecture/operations.md`.

O3 the legacy patch ships with everything at the first push, pushed earlier only if the intents' end comes too close. O4 MySQL follows the current LTS (8.4 now; the stack, the backup service and the tests switched), and so does Node (26 once it is LTS this month); in general every dependency is kept up to date. ## From Phase 8M-1 (the web server, Discord login and the public map, 2026-10-16)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-16-phase-8m1-web-server-and-map.md`, tasks 36c68de9..0edce40a, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: two Important, eight Minor, all folded in; final review on Opus: ready, seven Minor, fixed in 72e1765f..2e1074df, re-reviewed ready). Check, build, `web:build` and database suite green; timer files 10/10. Migration `2026_10_16_015_web_session` (015 is this plan's: first merge wins). New packages: fastify, @fastify/cookie, @fastify/static, leaflet (@types/leaflet): **the main checkout needs `npm ci` after the fast-forward**. `/maps` changed its description: register the commands again at staging. Not yet on Discord or in a browser: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-8m1-staging.md`; the setup guide `docs/web-setup.md`.

- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away, 2026-10-16):** the spec's packages installed without a separate go-ahead (the `wayfarer` worktree has its own `node_modules`); setting names `web.host`/`port`/`publicUrl`/`trustProxy`/`tiles` (agreed with the Docker session) instead of the spec's `listen`; only gyms in the server's regions are on the map (the spec says every gym; a gym in no region belongs to no channel); a login link opens a "Continue as <account>" page and only its POST (Origin checked) logs in, so a link previewer cannot use it up or catch the session, and a member sees whose account a pasted link would log them into; the link's address carries the server (`/auth/link/<guild>/<token>`); the client address is `request.ip` through trusted (loopback or private) hops of `X-Forwarded-For`, never `CF-Connecting-IP`; a gym's popup shows description and notice separately; cookies are `__Host-pine_session` and `__Secure-pine_oauth` over https (plain names on http localhost).
- **For the Docker plan (9A):** `dist/web/` is read relative to the working directory: the image must carry `dist/web/assets/` and `dist/web/assets.json` (`npm run build` makes them); without them the map page answers 503. In Compose, `web.host: 0.0.0.0` with the port unpublished and `trustProxy: true` (the tunnel's peer is a private address).
- **Only staging can show:** Leaflet under the CSP; Google's tiles through the Map Tiles API and its attribution (whether Google's logo image must be bundled: their policies ask for it "whenever possible"; the page shows the words "Google Maps" and the view's copyright); the real OAuth flow with the dev application (Matt registers `<publicUrl>/auth/callback` and sets `DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET`); that the chosen tunnel sends `X-Forwarded-For` (Tailscale Funnel especially), else every visitor shares one rate-limit bucket.
- **Left open (Minor):** `logOut` counts a 1xx/3xx status as success (cannot happen today: the route answers 204 and `fetch` follows redirects) (closed by 8M-6); the CSRF key is made at start, so open pages fetch a new token after a restart (the logout retries once); gyms in no region are not on the map (8M-2's editor shows them as warnings); about 126 `pine-web-*` folders in the OS temp directory from test runs before the fix (safe to delete when no suite runs).
- **Next for this work:** 8W-2 (stops and power spots), then 8M-2 (the region editor, which needs 8W-2's Wayspots), 8M-3..5.

## From Phase 8W-2 (every Wayspot kept, the Wayspot's picture and address in posts, `/wild near:`, 2026-10-16)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-16-phase-8w2-wayspots.md`, tasks 497e9d2f..3ee3155b, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: two Important, twelve Minor, all folded in with Matt's additions; final review on Opus: ready, six Minor, fixed in d865ba7f..69681e85, re-reviewed). Migration `2026_10_16_017_wayspot` (015 is 8M-1's; 8H's is `2026_10_16_018_party_history`, 016 stays unused). `/wild` has a new option: register the commands again at staging. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-8w2-staging.md`.

- **Matt's (2026-10-16, after mock-ups of a raid at Temple Sinai):** layout C — a raid post's and a train's current-stop location block shows the gym's address line under **Location Information**, then its notes, the Wayfarer picture beside them, "(no address available)" when there is a picture but no address; the `/wild near:` line carries the place's address, so members need not open the map; pictures asked for at `=s400` (the bare address gives 512 px; Temple Sinai's original is 2432×4320, 4.4 MB).
- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away):** `/wild near:` designed here from Matt's search notes (optional `near` after `details`; typed text acts only on exactly one hit that matched every word as a whole word, across every kind and region; otherwise five suggestions); the table also keeps Wayfarer gyms Pine did not take (kind `gym`, for 8M-2) and a `formerKind`; a row's `lastSeen` is its file's scan time, clamped to now, and an older file neither overwrites a newer row nor deletes one seen after its scan began (a row of the file's own scan time is its own); pictures only from `lh3.googleusercontent.com` (http shown by https); a Wayspot's address in the form a gym's address line takes ("20 Market Street, Pinewood, PA 15999, USA"; street and town only would be a small change in `wayspotAddressLine`); a location block appears only with notes or a picture (an address alone makes none).
- **Migrations (agreed with the cutover session):** first merge wins, and whoever merges second renumbers above it; never run a branch's unmerged migration on the dev database — stage a migration only from `typescript-migration`, or from a branch whose number already sorts after everything merged (Kysely refuses a name that sorts before one it has run).
- **Only staging can show:** the directory's region placement with Pittsburgh's 28 outlines (untimed); a city import's Wayspot write (1.9 s for 43,057 rows on the test container); the first `near:` search before the indexes are built (gyms only); the pictures and addresses in real posts and `/wild` reports.
- **Left open (Minor):** "libary" ranks "Vicary House" before real libraries (the gym search's own two-typo correction); a stop titled "Little Free Libary" is an as-typed match, so the real libraries are not offered for that spelling; `planKept`'s guard against older files is in the plan only (one admin lock covers every import); a preview whose read of the kept rows fails offers no Import when only stops and power spots would change; an older file can add back a Wayspot a newer scan no longer had (a missing row has no `lastSeen`); `/wild` resolves typed text in the guard and again in `execute` (two searches; guards pass nothing to `execute`); the `/wild near:` line keeps "(no address available)" in a report re-sent without pictures. (closed by 8W-3; an exactly typed oddly spelt stop is still a clear hit, ruling)
- **Next for this work:** 8M-2 (the region editor, with the Wayspots as layers), then 8M-3..5.

## From Phase 8C (the cutover tools, 2026-10-16)

- **Built:** plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-8c-cutover-tools.md`, tasks 75d1a53..2079a04, fixes 5430fbf, 7a71e79, merged b2fe788. `./pine emoji-upload`, `role-auth-export`, `convert-settings`, `cutover-check`, `deploy-commands --rehearsal / --clear-guild`; rehearsal mode (`rehearsal` in the settings); `/admin cleanup-legacy`; the runbook `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase-8c-cutover-runbook.md`. Conventions in `docs/architecture/operations.md`.
- **Decisions for Matt to look at:** in a rehearsal every answer outside the rehearsed forums is private, the screenshot mention trigger is off, `/request` is refused, the web pages and the tunnel stay off; the rehearsal forum is created hidden with its whole overwrite set; the converter keeps every legacy value the new bot reads (`features.*` missing as `false`), never prints a secret, never writes a blank `BACKUP_DIR`; a cleanup run deletes at most 400 channels (run it again for the rest).
- **Done 2026-10-16 (merge ec30a7f, commits 8d0fc1c, cf7de88, 2228907 after two focused Opus checks):** the list below, and from those checks: a refused hide of a forum that was already there counts as hidden only when its overwrites hide it (`isHiddenForum`: @everyone denied View with its own server permissions counted; View allowed only to the bot, its own managed role, staff roles and the testers), the moderator's answer names the permissions the bot lacks and says exactly what a hand-hidden forum must allow, `cutover-check` in rehearsal mode FAILs on `HIDDEN_FORUM_NEEDS` missing from the bot's own roles (one line per problem, except a missing required permission such as Manage Roles, which keeps its general FAIL beside the rehearsal one). F1 chose "taken as hidden" (the creation already set the whole hidden set); F6 is a line of the answer plus a WARN, not a user error; the shutdown order (F8) is not pinned by a test (the steps are inline in `startApp`).
- **Was before the rehearsal (the last review, all Minor/Nit):** F1 a refused second hide of a new hidden forum (a category-only bit such as Add Reactions denied) should be taken as "hidden" and posting go on, or the reason name the category; F2 cutover-check in rehearsal mode requires Manage Roles from the bot's role (not only through the category); F3 the fake gateway refuses Manage Roles on any overwrite of a non-admin bot, requires Manage Roles (and Manage Channels on create), computes `hideForum`'s bits from the category, and a test shows a category-only bit added by the PATCH; F4 the non-admin forum test also shows the summary posted; F5 `testing.md` describes `FakePartyGateway.botPermissions` and `FakeDiscordApi.globalCommands`; F6 `unmatchedRehearsedRegions` caught in `/admin setup-forums`; F7 `HIDDEN_FORUM_REFUSED` only when a forum was being made; F8 the "rehearsal count" shutdown step after "discord"; F9 the global-commands read in cutover-check in a try of its own (a WARN); F10 runbook R1's non-admin step: rehearse a region without a forum, take Administrator off the bot's role, and how to undo (delete the forum, clear `forumChannelId`, give the role back).
- **Only Discord shows:** whether an Integrations app-level allow beats `default_member_permissions` for one member (runbook R1, on the dev application first); a hidden forum made by a non-admin bot role with the category's bits added afterwards (R1 staging step before R5).

- **Decisions for Matt to look at:** endings `completed` (somebody completed / was there, whatever closed it), `expired`, `cancelled`, `cutover`, `unknown`; the sweep (at start and hourly) records ended parties left without a record as `unknown`, skips parties whose own retry is pending, logs and leaves unreadable rows; mentions in names, descriptions and labels become `@member` (role and channel mentions kept); the import takes the server from the regions that matched (or `--server`), gyms only on a takeover database, one bad record never ends the run; test users of the throwaway test MySQL get PROCESS (the lock-wait test reads `information_schema`).

## Meetup safety: no meetups in residential areas without a moderator (research 2026-10-17, plans 8S-1 and 8S-2 after 8D)

- **Research:** https://claude.ai/artifact/VLqQhTqtYy6PqW7BNK8V5W (squirrelbreeze, east-shadyland, oakland; scripts in that session's scratchpad). City of Pittsburgh zoning (WPRDC, residential = R1D, R1A, R2, R3, RM, RP, RIV-RM; hillside H counted with them) plus OpenStreetMap public places (parks, playgrounds, pitches, community gardens, schools, universities, places of worship, libraries, community centres, cemeteries, museums, shops, cafés, restaurants, stations; private gardens left out) within 60 m, plus a Wayspot whose title names a public kind of place unless it names a yard feature (little free library, apartments, home business). Of 1,874 Wayspots 233 (12 %) would need review, most of them rightly; zoning alone would have flagged 370.
- **Matt's rules (2026-10-17):** green and blue Wayspots (allowed, or kept by an exemption) are allowed; a red one is not refused but needs a moderator's approval, and an approval whitelists it for good. An allowed place has a Report button that sends it to the moderators for a refusal review (refused places are blacklisted). A pin is allowed in a non-residential zone or inside a public place; elsewhere it too can be sent for approval, which is one time only (never whitelisted). Meetups only (not trains). The member is told only that the place "needs a moderator's approval", never why. The moderators' channel is configurable with a recommended default; a request expires after 24-48 hours.
- **Proposed defaults:** `settings.channels.moderation`, a staff-only channel; never the bot updates channel, which is public (Matt). Unset: meetup safety stays off (every place allowed as today), the start logs a warning and `cutover-check` warns. A request expires after 48 hours or an hour before the meetup's start, whichever is first.
- **Shape:** 8S-1: the safety data built offline per server from the region outlines (`safety:build`, zoning source configurable, OSM land use as the fallback outside cities that publish zoning; polygons in a table with a spatial index), a stored verdict per Wayspot (recomputed on rebuild and on `/gym import`), place rulings (allow / refuse, by whom, when), the pure check (allowed / needs review / refused), and the review queue (requests in the database, Approve / Approve once / Refuse with "refuse from now on", the member told by DM, expiry). 8S-2: Report place (throttled, one per member and place), `/admin places` (rulings, pending requests, undo), verdicts on the web map. ODbL attribution for the OSM data where it is shown.
- **Superseded by the design (Matt, 2026-10-17, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-meetup-safety-design.md`):** the lighter version was built. There is no zoning, OSM data or web map layer.
  - Every pin needs a moderator.
  - A Wayspot whose name looks like a front-yard feature needs a moderator.
  - Staff skip the check.
  - `/meetup` asks for the place, the start and the information.
  - Blocks come with appeals.
  - If pin requests swamp the moderators, the zoning version above is the next step.

- **Next for this work:** 8M-3 (parties on the map and joining: an ended party's `Party` row is deleted once its history is written, 8H — never read an ended party by id), 8M-4, 8M-5; the staging of 8W-1, 8W-1R, 8W-2, the parts of 8M-1/8M-2 that need Matt (OAuth, drawing by hand) and 8M-2F.

## The #bot-updates gym summary wording (2026-10-17)

Matt's request (2026-10-16, via the cutover session): the summary says what changed, not the source. Commits 6a49e607 and 7cdc4ca9 on `wayfarer`, reviewed (Sonnet): "Professor Pine has added 30 gyms, renamed 1, and moved 1." (serial comma, zero counts left out), then "It also added 120 PokéStops and 3 power spots, changed 4 PokéStops, and removed 2 power spots."; an import that changed only PokéStops or power spots posts under "The PokéStops and power spots changed"; a stop counts as changed only for a new title, kind or a move over 10 m (a new address or picture alone is silent); a stop that became a gym is not counted as removed. **For Matt to look at:** whether a stop-only import should post at all (one condition, `anyChanges`). Check 6,951, build, db 417.

## From Phase 8D (posts with a "Where" section, meetups with a place, pins from maps links, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8d-post-where.md`, tasks 6882ab85..467ad679, each equal to its prototype commit; mockup approved by Matt in four rounds, https://claude.ai/artifact/JpEZbcPn3N3jGN1ZA7nRJV; prototype review by three Opus reviewers, two Important and many Minor, folded in, re-reviewed, one more Important folded in; whole-branch review (Opus, high): ready to merge, Minor 8D-1 to 8D-6, fixed in dafa5a0a and 6a80e2da). Check 7,022, build, database suite 418 green. No new package, no migration; party state document version 10 (`meetup.place`; version 9 read with none). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase-8d-staging.md` (it starts with `npm run deploy-commands -- --global`: `/meetup` gains `near`, help texts changed).

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-16/17):** the post's heading block says what the party is (the boss's lines in it, the gym's name out of the first line); **Where** has the place's name plain bold, its address and one Map link, the notice, the Wayfarer picture beside; no Map button in the bottom row; no elite raid gym mark anywhere members see (data, staff views, `#elite-gym-raids` copies and the Elite raid tier stay); a train shows the next two stops with a count (past the end for a loop or turn-back, "then back here" one stop before a turn), a route of three stops or fewer whole, and a "Whole route" button (`party:<id>:route`, `/route show`'s text privately) on longer routes; a meetup is near a gym, PokéStop, power spot or a pin, a snapshot set once by `/meetup near:` and never changed, shown live while the place stays within 25 m (gym edits and Wayspot loads re-render the post); a pin is named by the nearest place within 50 m, else the link's place name, else "Dropped pin"; maps links and coordinates are read wherever a place is named (`/wild near:` too). From the meetup safety work: `/location` stays for raids, meetups have no free-text "where" (details in `/info`).
- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was asleep):** Move train… stays on a finished route (Previous and Reverse undo a Finish; mockup 7b drew it without); a turn-back train before its turn: ", and N more to the end, where it turns back"; a finished short route drops its mode words; short links (`maps.app.goo.gl`, `goo.gl/maps`, `maps.apple/p`, `osm.org/go`) are refused with advice (open it and copy the address, or paste a dropped pin's coordinates), never followed; links that show no one point (searches, Street View, `map_action=map`, Apple's `q=words`, `geo:0,0?q=…`) are refused; a Google link's pin (`!3d!4d`) wins over the map's centre; Wayspots without a title are never offered or named; titles are cut to whole characters (255, link names 100); the start-time DM of a meetup ends "Where: …" as the post shows the place now.
- **Not done (follow-ups):** the party history does not record a meetup's place; autocomplete offers no "📍 pin" choice for pasted coordinates; the live map does not show a meetup's place (8M-5 can, and can set a pin); no DM shows a meetup's brief yet (its Map is seen nowhere); Google satellite-view links (`@lat,lon,NNNm` without `!3d…!4d`) are refused as no point although a map's centre otherwise counts (`readLocation`'s `\d+z` pattern); whether a train's region copy should stay once its route was cleared (staging step 20 shows what happens).
- **Rollback:** once the bot writes version 10 documents, older code cannot read those meetups.
- **Next for this work:** the staging run (checklist above); plan 8S (meetup safety) builds on the meetup's place and reshapes `/meetup`; 8M-4 builds on the pin reader (`src/domain/geo/pin-input.ts`).

## From the staging run of 8D (2026-10-17, both accounts)

On typescript-migration 52ae0174 (8D, 8M-4, 8M-4S), commands deployed globally; notes in the git-ignored `var/stage/results-8d.md`.

- **Fixed during the run (3f23c84a):** the bot did not start: the kept Wayspots load before the parties, and 8D's re-render of meetups near a PokéStop read the store before it was loaded. `PartyStore.isLoaded`; a load before the parties re-renders nothing.
- **Passed:** raid and egg posts (heading block with the boss, Where with name, address, Map, notice and picture; no Map button below); no elite mark for members while the elite copy and `/gym show` keep it; remote and elite copies; trains of 7 stops walked through every mode (every Next line as mockup cards 7, 7b, 7c, "then back here" one stop before a turn), Finish and Previous, short routes listed whole, no route, Whole route in the post and on a region copy; a train's region copy without buttons on a short route, and taken down once the route was cleared (settles the checklist's open note); meetups near a PokéStop, a gym (nickname in the snapshot, renames and notices shown live, the snapshot again after a move over 25 m) and at typed coordinates; `/wild near:` with a Google Maps place link (named by the link) and the refusals of a short link, a search link and a pin outside the regions; the DMs (a raid's Map button; a meetup's start-time DM ending "Where: …"); a restart reading every version 10 meetup; `/help wild`, `/help meetup`, `/help route show`.
- **Done in 8D-F (phone, F2, moderate, from 8A):** on the phone a status section whose text has two lines (the status and the names) puts its button under the text; one-line sections keep it at the right. Fixed as Matt's mockup: a section holds a status's heading alone, or a group's heading line with its Join, and the names (a group's lobby note and status lines) are a text display under it (`PostBlock` `details`). Past `MAX_COMPONENTS` `postMessage` puts details back into their sections, last first and as few as needed, before it drops the gym's picture: the worst raid (five groups, every control, both pictures) would hold 44 and keeps only group A's lines apart (40); the worst train 43, groups C to E folded (40); one group never folds (35 at most in the tests).
- **Done in 8D-F (phone, F3, minor):** the egg thumbnail was cut at the bottom on the phone: PokeMiners' egg icons are 112×128 and the phone crops thumbnails to squares. The eight egg pictures are padded to transparent 128 squares once (`npm run eggs:make`) and committed in `pictures/eggs/` (in the image too). With the web pages at a public address the bot serves them (`/eggs/<name>.png`); without them (or on `localhost`) a post carries the egg as a file (`attachment://`), uploaded again with every edit while it is an egg and taken away by the edit at hatch; copies and DMs likewise. `settings.raidEggPicturesUrl` is retired. A file Discord refuses (no Attach Files: 50013) leaves the egg out, and the channel is remembered ten minutes (`RefusedFiles`); with the web pages the address waits for the server to listen and carries `?v=<file hash>` (review of 8D-F). Open for staging: whether Discord takes an `attachment://` thumbnail in a forum post's first message and in a conversion, and whether the thumbnail flickers when an edit uploads the egg again (step 38). If it flickers, the alternative is per message: keep the egg's attachment by its id (`attachments: [{ id }]`, the id from the answer to the send, kept per message) and upload it only when the message has none, which Discord's docs allow for keeping a file but do not say an `attachment://` component may then still name it.
- **Done in 8D-F (F4, Matt, 2026-10-17):** a raid's post says how to set a meeting time while it has none: one group ``-# No meeting time yet: `/meet` sets one.`` (under its label, or a text of its own), several groups one line after them, ``-# `/meet` sets your group's meeting time.`` (Matt's "a group's" made "your group's": `/meet` sets the member's own group's), while one has none; not once the raid is over or closed, not on meetups or trains. Commands in backticks as the train's "No time has been set yet" line has them.
- **Side effect:** moving Cathedral of Learning away and back had Google fetch its address anew ("4400 Fifth Ave" for "100 Lytton Avenue"); its nickname "Cathy" and a notice were set for the test.
- **Not staged:** an old-code meetup (none in the dev database), gyms without an address (none on the test server), a 30-stop Whole route, the dropped-pin DM wording, `/targets`, `/help find-gym`, `/help gym elite`, an import renaming a stop under a meetup (tests only), the longest maps link.
