## From 8D-G (status buttons as icons, legacy Pine's reaction icons, one bottom row, 2026-10-17)

The phone staging of 8D-F showed "🤔 Interested" too wide to stay beside its line. Matt's decisions (mockup version 3, and two additions the same day): the four status buttons are icons without labels, a group's Join is its letter with Join's icon, the icons are legacy Pine's reaction icons as application emoji (`pinejoin`, `pinehere`, `pinedone`, `pineremote`, `pineleave`; Interested keeps 🤔, −1 and +1 their arrows; Leave and Remote keep their words), and the buttons below the lists are one row (Set time joins Leave, −1, +1, Remote; a sixth button starts a second), for raids, meetups and trains alike, their copies included. The icons are copies of the legacy `assets/images/reactions/` in `pictures/emoji/` (in the image). Not yet on Discord: staging steps 42 to 46 of `2026-10-17-phase-8d-staging.md`. The Opus review asked for a fix wave first (G-1 to G-5): the buttons take only the application's emoji (a server's cache goes stale, and a button with an emoji Discord refuses has the whole post refused), and the bare form of a post or a copy shows the plain emoji, so a refused body degrades instead of failing; `controlEmoji` gives the plain emoji for anything that is not a custom emoji's text; the guide and announcements describe several groups; once uploaded, `pineremote` is also the remote marker in the lists.

- **Needs Matt:** the upload (`npm run emoji:upload`, then `-- --apply`) on the dev application, and on the production application before the cutover; until then `cutover-check` warns that five of the game's icons are missing (they count among `GAME_ICON_NAMES`), and the posts show the plain emoji (🏃 ✅ 🎉 👋 📡); a legacy server's own `pinejoin` and the rest are never used on buttons, only in text.
- **Accessible name:** Discord's button has no field for one besides its label, so an icon-only button is read by its emoji's name (`pinejoin`, or the character's name) after the line beside it. Whether a screen reader says anything useful is staging step 43; if not, a readable emoji name (`join`) would need a rename of the uploaded emoji and of `CONTROL_ICON_NAMES`.
- **Left open:** `CLAUDE.md`'s line for `npm run emoji:upload` still says "type, weather and team icons" (Matt edits it); the copies' log line still says "Discord refused the picture of a copy" when it was a button's emoji (the post's says both); the web map's client says of a group's button "as the post has it: `Join B`" (`src/web/client/parties.ts`, left alone as the brief asked: the map keeps its words); the guide's table of commands (`docs/guides/migrating-a-legacy-instance.md`) has two rows wider than its columns (Markdown renders it; Prettier does not format `.md` here).
- **From the re-review of 8D-G's fixes (ready to merge; Minor, left open):** a refused button emoji has no memory (after an application emoji is deleted, each new state of each post and copy costs a refused call and a warning until the hourly re-read: start `appEmoji.refresh()` rate-limited on such a fallback); pictures and emoji are dropped together in the bare form (a gym picture Discord keeps refusing also takes the icons off its buttons; an in-between form would keep them); the fake's name-only emoji check is not anchored to one emoji grapheme (`pine🏃` passes, keycaps fail); a copy's warning still says "picture" when an emoji was refused; no test of an existing copy's edit with a refused emoji. `CLAUDE.md`'s `emoji:upload` line was brought up to date at the merge.

## From the phone staging of 8D-F and 8D-G (2026-10-17, both accounts)

On typescript-migration c532fa4b; commands deployed globally; `emoji:upload --apply` on the dev application (the five icons; `pinedone` uploaded again after step 46); notes in the git-ignored `var/stage/results-8d.md`. Web pages at a local address, so eggs went as attached files.

- **Passed (Matt's phone):** every status button an icon at the right of its line, names under the lines, the egg whole, the hint above the statuses; two groups with the longest heading ("Group B: by the fountain · 1 trainer · meeting at …") on one line beside its button; one hint line for several groups; no hint in the remote copy; the bottom row wrapped by the phone (Leave, −1, +1, then Remote). Matt: "looks basically acceptable".
- **Finding F4 (moderate; the re-review's G-R1 and G-R2 seen on Discord):** a deleted application emoji (`pinedone`, deleted in the Developer Portal) made Discord refuse every edit (`COMPONENT_INVALID_EMOJI`); the bare form kept the post and copies updating with plain emoji, but it also dropped every picture (egg, boss, gym) until the hourly re-read of the application's emoji, with a refused call and a warning per edit. Fix: an in-between form (plain emoji, pictures kept) before the bare one, and an immediate rate-limited re-read of the application's emoji on such a refusal.
- **Done in 8D-H (F4, with G-R1, G-R2 and G-R4; 3cfda7f0, 3cc3d8bf, 562bafc7, docs d22602ac; the review's fix wave, H-1 to H-7, in 6d8bf218, docs f6ef171b):** a message is tried in the forms of a `FormTrail` (`src/services/refused-files.ts`): as it is, without its files, with plain button emoji and its pictures kept, bare; a form identical to one tried is left out, none is tried twice (at most four sends), for post creation (both ways), `editPostOf` and the copies. `refusedPart` (`src/discord/party-gateway.ts`) reads the fields of Invalid Form Body: an emoji Discord names (a field under `emoji`, or a code with `EMOJI`, also in the message) goes straight to the plain emoji, files and pictures kept, and the pictures go only if that is refused too; a picture it names (a field under `media`) goes to the form without pictures with the custom emoji kept (a fifth form the brief left to "where the forms allow it"), then bare; a refusal it does not explain goes to the plain emoji first (after the files, for a message that carries them), then bare. What Discord answers for a refused picture is not known (its code is not documented; the fake names `….accessory.media.url`): a real picture refusal without that field takes the unnamed way, which still ends bare. A named emoji refusal calls `ApplicationEmoji.refused()`: one re-read at once, at most one a minute (`REFUSED_REREAD_GAP_MS`), logged, whose change shows the posts anew (`emojiChanged`). The warnings say what was taken to be refused and what the next form newly leaves out (`refusalSaid`, `FormStep.dropped`), the copies' as the post's. Staging step 46 rewritten for it. Still open from the re-review: no test of an existing copy's edit with a refused emoji (a copy's send is tested; the edit goes through the same `sendInForms`).
- **Step 46 run again on 8D-H (2026-10-17, typescript-migration eeaa8013): PASS.** Matt deleted `pinejoin` while the bot ran; a press of Join on the #remote-raids copy of a new T3 egg (Sixth Presbyterian Church): one warning "Discord refused an emoji of a button; editing with plain emoji" for the post, one "Discord refused an emoji of the application; reading them anew". The post and the copy kept the egg and the gym's picture, 🏃 on Join and the four remaining custom icons (the re-read was done before the copy's edit, so the copy went out without a refusal). A second press: no refusal, no warning, no second re-read. `pinejoin` uploaded again (`emoji:upload --apply`).
- **Not stageable here:** a refused file (step 40): Attach Files denied at server level did not stop the bot (Administrator or an overwrite); tests only. Eggs from a public web address (step 37) need the tunnel.


## From Phase 8T (splitting a group off a raid train, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8t-train-split.md`, tasks f7d89fa0..bac44ce4, each equal to its prototype commit; design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-train-split-design.md`, mockup approved by Matt in chat; prototype review by three Opus reviewers (three Important, the rest Minor), folded in over three fix rounds, each re-reviewed; whole-branch review (Opus, high): ready to merge, Minor 8T-1 to 8T-4, fixed in f6b5040c..609827b2). Check 7,489, build, database suite 440 green. No new package. Migration 020 (`PartyHistory.splitFromId` with its index, `PartyHistoryAttendee.splitOff`; additive, guarded); party state document version 11 (`train.splitFrom` with `pending`, `train.splitOff`; version 10 read with neither). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase-8t-staging.md` (it starts with `npm run deploy-commands -- --global`: `/group` gains `split`; the dev database has 019).

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-17):** no per-group positions: a group that wants its own pace splits off into a train of its own, often before the train starts, to carry the planned route; the conductor may split any group, a group's member their own unless the conductor is in it; a grey "Split group…" button by "Move train…" and `/group split`, both opening a private "are you sure?"; the new train is named `<name> (<group label or letter>)` and keeps route, stop, mode, boss and start; one link to the other train in each post's small print, the thread line naming only the doer; the members who moved are pinged in the new post; each train's history records what happened on it (the group on the old train until the split, the new train from the split); a split that would leave the old train empty is refused.
- **Rulings for Matt to look at:** staff may always split; the new train's conductor is the old conductor when they went with the group, else the presser when in the group, else none; the old train keeps its other groups' letters, and a new group never takes a letter in use and prefers one no split named; the button only while two groups have members, not on region copies; the ping honours `mentions`; at most 10 splits kept in a train's small print; empty groups and trains without a post cannot be split; links are `<#post>` mentions; with an Anonymous presser the old post's line still names them unless they made that train (`doer`).
- **The window of a restart:** closed by 8T-F (below): both trains are written in one transaction.
- **Migration 020 and the web branch:** `wayfarer` (8M-5M) also numbered a migration 020 (`meetup_request_source`); 8T merged first, so it becomes 021 there (the web session was told at merge). On a database where 020 ran before 019, the checklist's step 2 has the way out.
- **Not done (follow-ups):** splitting from the live map (one code path ready: `splitGroupAs`; the store refuses a stale group as `group-changed`, which a route could map to 409); merging two trains back together.
- **Only staging can show:** the confirmation and the "Splitting…" note on a phone; the `<#post>` links in the small print of a forum post and its copies; the ping in the new post; the live map showing both trains.
- **Rollback:** once the bot writes version 11 documents, older code cannot read those parties; 020 is additive.

## From Phase 8T-F (a train split as one transaction, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8t-f-split-transaction.md`, tasks 79f734e9..13bf06d4, each equal to its prototype commit; brief approved by Matt in chat: "Sounds like we should do transactional"; prototype review (Opus): ready, one minor and four nits folded in; whole-branch review (Opus, high): ready, findings 8T-F-1 to 8T-F-6 fixed in a2c9e651..a59eabf0). Check 7,587, build, database suite 448 green. No new package, no migration, party state still version 11.

- **What changed:** `PartyStore.updateBoth(ids, change)` (both parties' locks, lowest id first; one `PartyStorage.saveBoth` in one transaction; memory and listeners only after the commit) carries a split's two writes; no carry-over write, no retry. A pending new train only means "its post exists and the split never committed": it is taken back at start and by the upkeep. A train still pending cannot be split itself (`split-pending`; found by the whole-branch review: its members could have ended on two trains).
- **Rulings for Matt to look at:** a press on the party with the lower id waits while the pair is written; a pending train whose old train ended since is taken back (it used to be settled); a pending train its still-active old train lists (only 8T's first build could leave one) is settled as stored and logged as an error.
- **Accepted risk (documented, `trains.md`):** a commit whose acknowledgement is lost (the connection drops after MySQL committed) makes the split's take-back leave the group on neither train in the database until the old train's next write, for good after a restart before it; the same rare risk as any `update`.
- **Staging:** 8T's checklist (steps 3 and 25 updated) covers it; nothing more only staging can show.

## Max battles (designed 2026-10-20; Gantt phase 15, plans 8X-1 to 8X-6)

Design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-max-battles-design.md`, approved by Matt 2026-10-20 after a brainstorm in chat and a fresh review (two Blockers, twelve Important, folded in); preliminary plan `2026-10-17-max-battles-preliminary.md`. A max battle is a fourth party kind at a Power Spot (or an "Unlisted power spot" pin), reported with `/max` and from the map, ending at 21:00; max bosses from Pokebattler's `/raids` with ScrapedDuck's Max Monday and Max Battle Day events featuring their boss; lobby sizes 4 / 100 with Join filling groups for max battles and Gigantamax trains only; `/shout group:`; trains with a kind (`/train kind:`, the Move train panel) and stops that are gyms or Power Spots (`/route add stop:`, ordered by the train's kind); species wants cover max battles, one DM an hour per member and boss. Order: 8X-1 and 8X-2, then 8X-3 and 8X-4, then 8X-5 (the map, with the web session) and 8X-6.

- **Left for later (Matt, 2026-10-20):**
  - **Screenshots of max battles**, which first needs the screenshot reader to tell what kind of screenshot it was given (raid, max battle, other), in passive mode too (the privileged intent on small instances). Undecided: how picky `/raid` should be with a max battle screenshot. (The reader part is done, 2026-10-22; the flow is designed in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-max-battle-screenshots-design.md`, a plan after 8X.)
  - The Move train panel does more than move the train once it has the kind menu: a better name for its button (Claude's suggestion: "Manage train…").
  - An unlisted gym as a pin, as an unlisted Power Spot is (Matt: "could arguably do this for gyms too").
  - Pokebattler as a fallback source of raid bosses when ScrapedDuck is stale (on 2026-10-20 it listed more T1 bosses and Mega Charizard X/Y beside ScrapedDuck's Mega Dragonite: it seems to keep bosses without an end date).
  - Spot targets ("max battles at this spot"), catch CP and moves for max bosses, a group menu and more than five groups (Matt: dropped "once we're full on groups").

## From the staging run of 8X-H (2026-10-23, 19:18 to 20:02, Googer driven by Claude, Matt at the Wi-Fi)

Staged with the checklist `2026-10-23-phase-8xh-staging.md` from typescript-migration at 881a767c (8X-H merged with the /rank work), logs `var/stage/logs/bot-8xh-1.log` and `bot-8xh-2.log` at debug, normal stage settings. **All run steps pass**; no ERROR line in the run.

- **Part one:** G1 passes (the refusal names ten movesets and ends ", and 90 more.", no hint; `moveset:Snarl / Blizzard` answers with counters). G2 passes on Train · Sweep turned back at its last stop: Move… offers "Next: turn back to …", "Previous: back to … (undo)" with its description line, then Skip; Previous keeps it heading for the end, Next from the last stop turns the route round; on a two-stop loop (Train · Loop Two) Move… offers no choice at either stop and one Submit moves once. G3 passes for Move… (the private answer "Move… takes it back to stop 2: …", the finished route said once, "Submit takes it back to stop 2, **…**"), Manage… → Finish it here and typed `/finish` ("`/previous` takes it back …"). G4 passes: the summary shows starts days ahead as Discord's `f` stamps ("October 26, 2026 at 7:00 PM") and one 4 hours ahead as "in 4 hours". G6 not seen (no 503); G7 nothing to do.
- **Part two:** a raid ending at 19:43 during an outage of about 15 minutes (Wi-Fi off 19:42 to 19:57, longer than the checklist's 5): one `discord shard error` at warn (ENOTFOUND) at 19:43:44, then `discord shard errors repeated` once a minute (1,612 in all); the raid's end, its archive warning (`could not do what the clock asked for`) and its closing (`could not show an ended party one last time`, `could not archive the post of an ended party`, the link line and the copy) all at warn; the boss sync and the research page failed at warn. The return: `discord gateway back` (awayMs 887,845, newSession true), `closed ended parties again` (redone 1, failed 0), the upkeep's debug lines, `caught up` with posts, upkeep, stop posts, raid bosses and research tasks under `ran` and nothing failed. The raid's post has no controls, the Ended tag, is closed and locked, no archive warning stands in it, its link line in #north-side is gone and the summary no longer lists it. Step 16: no routine resume in the run. Step 17 (10 s off): nothing at all in the log (the socket survived). Step 18 skipped. One Ctrl+C at the end: every shutdown step finished (party clock, catch-up), the crash marker gone.
- **Findings:**
  - **H1 (Important, operations): two bots ran at once.** At 19:17:17 a bot was started from somewhere unknown (node with the forward-slash path, its parent shell gone; Matt's cmd start came at 19:17:49). Both logged in with the dev token and answered the same interactions ("Unknown interaction" and "already acknowledged" on one press); the stale one, with its own parties in memory, undid Manage…'s turn-back with its Move. The second start also reported "restarted after a crash" and waited 30 s, because it found the first one's live marker. Suggestion: at the start, a marker whose `pid` is a live process (and the health file's heartbeat fresh) is another bot running from this folder: refuse to start, saying so, instead of reporting a crash. **Fixed** the same evening: `CrashRecord.runningElsewhere` (the marker's process alive, not this one, a heartbeat of it or its start within 90 s); the start logs fatal and exits 1, leaving the marker (`operations.md`).
  - **H2 (Minor, words):** Manage… → Finish it here answers "The route is finished. The train has finished its route of 2 stops. Move… takes it back …": the finish said twice. **Fixed:** the first sentence dropped (`managedWords`).
  - **Seen, not 8X-H:** the stage settings name `#meetup-review`, which the server lacks now (a warn at every start).
- **Left for Matt (his devices):** G2's description line under Previous on Android; G3 on the map (the train panel's Finish says "Previous takes it back …"); G4 on a device set to another time zone; step 18 (Ctrl+C right after a return) if wanted.
- **Left on the test server:** in #north-side, Train · Sweep (turns back, at stop 2 of the reversed route), Train · Loop Two (finished, start 23:45), Dynamax Duraludon at Allegheny Inn (until 21:00), the trains Planned and Wild Area.

## From Phase 8X-H (the 8X-G staging's findings and the catch-up on reconnect, closed 2026-10-23)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-23-phase-8xh-staging-fixes.md`, tasks b38acedd..da3d1e5d, each equal to its prototype commit; the brief read by a fresh Opus reviewer before any code: one Blocker, the rule of a real disconnect, and seven Important, folded in as R1–R17; prototype review by two Opus reviewers: nine Important (Discord REST's abort not counted as network, a third of routine resumes counted real, link lines and copies, a moderator's deletion, closings tied to the gateway, only network failures kept, shutdown, the checklist's timings) and Minor ones, then a fix review (C1: a pending deletion told as refused; five Minor), all folded in; typescript-migration merged in with 8X-S, bda676fb, conflicts in CLAUDE.md and the Gantt data only; whole-branch review on Opus, high: two Important (the checklist's outage missed the closing; a raid closed by the clock in an outage got no Ended tag on the redo) and six Minor, fixed in 3c87a7ce). Check (9,453 tests), build and database suite (597) green. No migration, no party state change, **no command change** (no deploy). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `2026-10-23-phase-8xh-staging.md` (part two takes the laptop's Wi-Fi away for five minutes, the log at debug).

- **Matt's answers (2026-10-23):** G4 a start or end more than a day ahead as Discord's date and time (each reader's time zone and DST); G2 Previous kept at a turn-back end as the undo ("Previous: back to X (undo)"), left out only on a two-stop loop; the closing of a party that ended during an outage redone (controls removed, Ended tag, leftovers, last words, a moderator's deletion); G1 cut to the shorter refusal (Mew is never a boss), the moveset menu unchanged. G7 needs no change: Pokebattler names no charged move for those slots (`MOVE_NONE`).
- **Claude's calls (Matt may object):** every active post redrawn on a real reconnect (unchanged ones not edited); "ends" follows "starts"; `/counters moveset:`'s autocomplete stays on the game master's list; the event pages with spawns are not in the catch-up (they retry within the hour); a sweep's non-Discord failures gathered into one warning (naming the first forum); a Discord 5xx counts as a network failure, a MySQL error never; closings redone in the hourly upkeep (one probe per run while the network is away, dropped after a day or when the post is gone), a redo on an archived post shows it first; a moderator's delayed deletion told "archived for now … I will try again within the hour"; the shutdown's steps within 150 s with 5 s to spare (party clock 6 s, catch-up 2 s, direct messages 12 s).
- **Accepted, not caught up:** last words sent whose answer was lost may be said twice by the redo; the clock's archive warning and completion question that fail in an outage are not sent again; failed DMs and post lines stay dropped; in Docker a DNS outage gives no shard errors (EAI_AGAIN), so only the time away or a new session tells a real outage there; in production the catch-up covers outages of about 30 s to 7 min (the watchdog restarts after that).
- **Left open:** in a report's decision, a party that ended between the press and the closing has its post deleted through `deletePostOf`, which still reads a network failure as Discord refusing ("delete it yourself"), and nothing redoes it (rare; a small fix when convenient).

## From the staging run of 8X-G (2026-10-23, from 04:00, Googer driven by Claude alone, later Tester by Matt)

Staged with the checklist `2026-10-22-phase-8xg-staging.md` from typescript-migration at 70f3f3e0 (8X-G and 8R-4L; the start moved the dev database to migration 030), log `var/stage/logs/bot-8xg-1.log` at debug. First pass without Tester and without a restart (Matt asleep), before 06:00, so no max battles.

- **Findings:**
  - **G1 (Minor, close to Important for Mew).** `/counters boss:Mew tier:T5` → "Change options…": "Boss moves" offers the average and the first 24 of the game master's 350 movesets, all `Pound / …` (`counters-dialog.ts:91`, `all.slice(0, MAX_OPTIONS - 1)`). Pokebattler simulates none of them, so every choice of the menu answers "Pokebattler has no results for Mew with Pound / Dark Pulse. It simulates Dragon Tail / Overheat, …" with a list of some 100 movesets cut off by "…". Typed `moveset:Snarl / Blizzard` works. Suggestion: where a boss has more movesets than fit, offer those of the kept answer (Pokebattler's own list, in its order) rather than the game master's first ones; and shorten the refusal's list.
  - **G2 (Minor).** At the last stop of a turn-back route Move… offers "Next: turn back to Olde Allegheny Gardens" and "Previous: Olde Allegheny Gardens", the same move twice: leave Previous out when it equals Next.
  - **G3 (Minor).** Move… on a finished route reads "The train has finished its route of 4 stops. Previous takes it back to stop 4: Allegheny Station." and then "Submit takes it back to stop 4, Allegheny Station.": the first sentence (the text of `/next`'s refusal) names Previous, which the dialog does not offer; say it once. The private answer of "Finish it here" ends the same way ("Previous takes it back …"), fine for `/previous` but the post has no Previous button.
  - **G5 (Minor, logging).** A DNS outage of the laptop (05:00:48 to about 05:02:40, `getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND gateway-us-east1-c.discord.gg` and `discord.com`) logged 206 `discord shard error` lines at ERROR in two minutes, besides 30 warnings "could not look for open posts of ended parties" (one per region); the bot came back by itself and answered on (Matt, 2026-10-23: an early test of what legacy Pine often needed a restart for). What the outage touched: the gateway reconnected at 05:02:35 with no restart and `/rank` uses were handled at 04:57–04:58 and again from 05:03:25; the raid boss sync (`could not sync the raid bosses`, FeedFailure ENOTFOUND), the research page and the application's emoji ("keeping the last list") failed once and kept what they had; the sweep of ended parties' posts failed for every region. Checked at 06:02: the research page was read again at 05:16 (`research tasks read`); the boss sync (hourly, next at about 06:01), the sweep and the emoji read log nothing when they succeed, so their rerun cannot be seen in the log: give each a debug line on success (with the catch-up below). **A second, harsher outage** (Matt: the laptop without any network for more than an hour, then on another network altogether, so both its local and public addresses changed): DNS failures from 08:14 (`ENOTFOUND gateway-us-east1-c.discord.gg`, 819 shard errors in all, some 115 a minute while awake, so the client retries about twice a second with no backoff), silence while the laptop slept (08:34 to 09:18), and the last errors at 09:18:2x; the same process answered `/help` at 09:28 and served the map's `/api/parties` at 09:26, no restart. **Decided (Matt, 2026-10-23): catch-up on reconnect, not a restart.** The health check never saw this outage (1 m 47 s, under its two minutes), and restarting on every reconnect would restart several times a day (Discord asks for routine reconnects) at the cost of a login, the posts redrawn and open dialogs failing. Instead, when the gateway is back after a real disconnect (a shard that was not Ready, not a routine resume), Pine runs at once what failed meanwhile rather than at its next interval: the sweep of ended parties' posts, the boss and max feeds, the research page and lineups, the application's emoji, and the summaries. The watchdog's restart after a long outage (`gateway` for five minutes) stays as it is. For the fix plan after this staging. Consider logging repeated shard errors of one kind once per minute (with a count), as an outage is not 206 errors.
  - **G4 (question).** The summary says "starts in 4 days" for a train starting 10-26 7pm, asked at 10-23 04:09 (3 days 15 hours): rounded up. Fine if meant.
- **Passed:** F2's other half (06:03): a Gigantamax Machamp battle at Allegheny Inn shows the Gigantamax Machamp picture; Gigantamax Pikachu is not in today's list, so its plain picture was not seen. M3 (`max events loaded` with max 6, big 2; `raid events read` with both). ## From Phase 8X-G (the 8X-F run's findings and the design talk, closed 2026-10-23)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-23-phase-8xg-design-talk.md`, tasks 80ba3b65..5039b3d9, each equal to its prototype commit; the brief read by a fresh Opus reviewer before any code: one Blocker, a radio group of one choice, and nine Important, folded in as R1–R18; Pokebattler probed with Matt's go-ahead (P1–P3, and its headers: `max-age=3600`, an ETag); prototype review by three Opus reviewers: two Important (the caching header, the which-group dialog's preselection against the default group) and sixteen Minor, then a fix review (six Minor), all folded in; typescript-migration merged in with 8R-4 and the security updates, 6a2b5ee0, conflicts in docs only; whole-branch review on Opus, high: one Important (staging step 17 could not reuse a letter) and six Minor, fixed in 9d5c23a9). Check (9,078 tests), build and database suite (583) green. No migration, no party state change (groups sorted when read); **commands change** (`/train start:` and `kind:`, `/end time:`, `/meetup start:`, `/admin setup-forums`): deploy the commands before staging. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `2026-10-22-phase-8xg-staging.md` (start the bot with `LOG_LEVEL=debug` for its steps 5 and 19).

- **For staging:**
  - Step 17 (a reused letter listed in its place) needs five accounts on the test server, one per group (an empty group goes); with fewer it cannot run on Discord, and the unit tests cover it.
  - The checklists before 8X-G name "Manage train…", its panel, "Split group…", "Whole route", "Set time" and "Active raids": read them as Move… and Manage… (the panel's moves in Move…; Reverse, Finish, the mode and the kind in Manage…), Split…, Route, Set time… and "Going on now".
  - Android's preselection with letter order: the which-group dialog lists the groups by letter and marks the default group (where Join, +1 and Remote put a newcomer; review r0 A2), so an iPhone starts there while Android picks group A by itself; Split…'s radio is in letter order with nothing marked (before, the largest group first and the presser's own last), so on Android the first group, often the presser's own, is picked by itself (review r0 A3). Check both on Android (Matt, 2026-10-22: letter order in the dialogs too; staging double-checks); a follow-up if a split is submitted by accident.
- **Left as they are (for Matt):**
  - F2: a Gigantamax boss PokeMiners has no icon for (Pikachu) shows its plain picture; no file name in no list is guessed (Matt, 2026-10-22). When PokeMiners lists one, `maxBossPicture` takes it with the next game-data download.
  - `/counters`' dialog offers the average and 24 of a boss's movesets; Mew has 100 (probe P1). `/counters moveset:` typed names any. A dialog cannot hold more without a second menu; not worth it until members ask.
  - Pokebattler takes no moveset and no smaller aggregation (P2, P3), so a raid answer is read whole (up to 32 MB) and reduced at once; the transient parse of a Mew-sized answer is some 73 MB of heap for some 270 ms (scratch run). Three requests at once could add up to some 250 MB in the worst case (three Mew-sized answers of other options at once); not seen, and the cache makes it rare.
  - The summary is drawn anew after every draw of the stops' post (review R11's choice: counts in the line); an edit goes out only where the line changed (the JSON of the summary is compared). The cost is one summary render per stops-post draw, in the region of the report.
  - Passing through counts a train while it will still reach a stop of the region (review r0 C4, Matt 2026-10-22: a train that finishes the stops from its current one on, a loop or turn-back every stop, a finished route none).
  - Pokebattler's answers are revalidated with their ETag after `max-age` (an hour); a 304 renews them without a download (review r0 A1).
  - Passing through links a train's copy in the region's channel by a message link, which needs the server's snowflake (`GuildRepository.snowflakeOf`, read once per server by the summary's render); `/status` in a region channel before the first summary render of that server since the start links the post instead.
  - The map's Manage train panel and its kind words are unchanged (the map's UX pass).
- **Decisions of the prototype** (the report lists them all; those Matt may want to see): "Passing through" counts in the summary's head (`1 train passing through`); the stops' line is the summary's last text, after a line; Manage…'s answer says what changed in the commands' and the map's words, and `Nothing changed.` where nothing was picked otherwise; a failure of Pokebattler gives the use back also for an answer too large or unreadable (R3 named the others); the train's third row is words only on a train, Group… included, while a raid's Group… keeps 👥.

## From the staging run of 8X-F (2026-10-22, afternoon and evening)

Staged with the checklist `2026-10-22-phase-8xf-staging.md` from typescript-migration at 7087fed8 (logs `var/stage/logs/bot-8xf-1.log`, and `bot-8xf-2.log` after a Docker restart), Googer driven by Claude, Tester and the phones (web, iPhone, Android) by Matt. Migration 029 ran at the first start. **Part one passes in full**: every finding of the 8X run is fixed. Part two: most 8X steps not run before now pass; what is left is listed below. The run's notes, step by step, are in the git-ignored `.superpowers/sdd/8x-f-staging-fixes/staging-run.md`.

- **Findings, for a small fix plan:**
  - **F1 (Important).** A `/counters` query Pokebattler has not computed lately can take more than the gateway's 10 seconds (`pokebattler-gateway.ts:153`): three times out of three new queries (SNORLAX_GIGANTAMAX and PIKACHU_GIGANTAMAX charger, MEW RAID_LEVEL_5), the retry later works. A timeout also pauses every Pokebattler request for 60 seconds, so one slow query blocks `/counters` for everyone for a minute. Recommendation: a longer timeout (the answer is deferred), no server-wide pause for one slow request, or one retry.
  - **F3 (Important).** `/counters boss:Mew tier:T5` can never answer: Pokebattler's answer passes the 5 MB cap (`pokebattler-gateway.ts:154`, reason `too-large`; its download is likely F1's first timeout too). 8X-6 step 9b fails on it; its moveset list itself is right (25: the average and 24 movesets). Recommendation: when a moveset is chosen, ask Pokebattler for that moveset only, if its API allows; otherwise a higher cap for raid simulations (check how the legacy bot asked).
  - **F2 (Minor, Matt).** A Gigantamax Pikachu battle's post shows the ordinary Pikachu picture, not a Gigantamax one.
  - **M1 (Minor).** `/train … start:` answers "`/meet` sets when it starts" although the start was given.
  - **M2 (Minor).** The unreadable-time refusal's example "10-17 6:15pm" is a past date (the option's hint says 10-24).
  - **M3 (Minor).** A successful `loadMax` logs nothing, so the checklist's "the log says the events were loaded" cannot be seen: an info line "max events loaded", as the other loads have.
- **Design questions for Matt** (the first three together, with Manage train…):
  - **Manage train… as a dialog: decided** (Matt, 2026-10-22). The rule is wider: every button or menu on a post that needs more input from the member opens a dialog, never a private reply with controls, which lands far down a busy post (a slash command's answer may keep its controls; Screenshot feedback stays a reply, as it must show the screenshot; Whole route only shows). Manage train… is the one post control left to change. **Superseded the same evening by two dialogs** (Matt): **Move…**, a dialog of Next: <stop> (pre-selected), Previous: <stop>, Skip <stop>, go to <stop>, Submit: two clicks per move, and closing it is "don't move"; **Manage…**, a dialog of the train's behavior only: "The route" (Keep it as it is, pre-selected; Reverse it; Finish it here), the end-of-route menu, the kind menu ("What the train is mainly for": Raids, Dynamax Battles, Gigantamax Battles). The third row's labels are shortened, as words, not icons (no picture members already know for these): Group…, Move…, Manage…, Split…, Route; a label ending in `…` opens a dialog (Matt: the cue to expect one), Route only shows. Both dialogs have the per-open token. **The `…` rule holds for every button** (Matt): Claude's audit of 2026-10-22 found five labelled buttons that open a dialog without it: "Set time" on a raid's post (→ "Set time…"), "Something is wrong" of screenshot feedback, "Appeal" of a meetup block's DM, "Ask the moderators" of a refused meetup place, and "Delete" of the moderators' pin-battle notice (each gets `…`); icon-only buttons that open a dialog (Meet's ⏰, and Join, Maybe, Here when a newcomer is asked which group) carry no label to mark. The rest already follow it (Group…, Split group…, Close…, Refuse place…, Block maker…, Remove all…, Change options…, the gym view's Edit…, Move…, Delete…, Add with details…, the editor's Save… and Fix…). The single dialog first agreed (kept for the record, mockup v2): the state line ("At stop 2 of 5: … Next: …"); "Move the train", a radio of Next: <stop>, Previous: <stop>, Skip <stop>, go to <stop> (today's Skip), Don't move, **with Don't move pre-selected** (Matt: an opening to change something else must never move the train and send the announcement); "The route", a radio of Keep it as it is (pre-selected), Reverse it, Finish it here (Matt: these change the train's behavior, they are not moves); the end-of-route menu; the kind menu, labelled "What the train is mainly for" with the choices Raids, Dynamax Battles, Gigantamax Battles (the posts keep "Raid Train" and so on); Submit applies what changed; the per-open token against stale choices. Reverse with a move: reversed first, then the move in the new direction; Finish with a move: refused ("Finish ends the route here, so choose Don't move"). A move takes three clicks (open, pick, Submit); the quick way stays `/next` (and `/previous`, `/skip`, `/finish`) typed in the post. Several moves in a row are rare (Matt); no extra button on the post.
  - **The kind's label: decided** (Matt, 2026-10-22): "What the train is mainly for", choices Raids, Dynamax Battles, Gigantamax Battles, in the dialog above and `/train kind:`'s hint. The talk before it ("What the train goes to"): Matt's "Primary stop type" says it is a focus, not a rule (a route may mix gyms and spots); "Train type" reads as a rule. Claude: "What the train is mainly for", with "stops can be gyms or power spots" in its explanation.
  - **The summary post: decided** (Matt, 2026-10-22): it lists every kind of party with a link to each post (it does already, with "…and N more"), so its title "Active raids" changes (Claude: "Going on now"); trains from other regions that stop in this one get a section of their own ("Passing through"); research and Rocket stay a post of their own, and the summary gets one line linking to it ("Today at the stops: 6 tasks, 2 hideouts"). Open: whether "Today at the stops" moves from the region's text channel into the forum. Claude's recommendation, to settle at the 8R staging: stay in the text channel (the forum's one pin is the summary's, so a forum post would sink as parties get activity; grunt messages are in the text channel too; built and linked from DMs already).
  - **Trains passing through** (the talk before it): a train from another region that stops in this one shows only as the copy in the region's text channel; the region's forum and its "Active raids" post do not show it. Claude's recommendation: a "Trains passing through" part in that post. Matt has more feedback on that post (it carries more than raids, so its name should change): a design talk first.
  - **Group order: decided** (Matt, 2026-10-22): groups always in letter order (A to E), in the post, the dialogs and on the map; today they are listed oldest first, so a reused letter comes after later ones (C D E A B).
  - Remote on a meetup's post (row 2): already answered (Matt, 2026-10-22, "From Phase 8X-5"): it stays for now (PvP tournaments were played fully remote); `remoteAllowed` decides it if he changes his mind.
- **For the map's UX pass** (Matt): a party's popup should be as close as possible to its Discord post, with the same interactions; that also gives the map a way to move to another group after joining (today the group is chosen only at Join).
- **Not faults, seen on the way:** Sableye, an upcoming feed boss (Max Monday 10-26), is read at its listed 3★ when typed in full although `/max`'s list hides it until then; Uxie before 10-24 is no boss now (feed start 10-24), so 8X-6 step 9 as written cannot pass before then (checked with Duraludon instead); the map's login was lost after the Claude app's restart (the browser pane's cookie; sessions are kept in the database); a double press of Reverse cannot reach the bot (Discord disables the button while the first press is in flight; the stale-panel refusal was seen instead).
- **Not run, for a later staging:**
  - 8X-2 5's warnings on a changed list and the 304 (run at 18:39 with `refreshMinutes: 5`: no error, no warning, `/admin boss sync max:True` "The max boss feed lists the same bosses as before."; an unchanged list logs nothing at info, so "max bosses updated" shows only when the list changes, and a 304 only at debug); 8X-2 9 passes (the shutdown at 18:38:58 finished every step within 55 ms, nothing after);
  - 8X-1B 18 (an elite raid and a raid's copy), Group…'s read-only mode on the map;
  - 8X-3 17a/b (the summary post: after the design talk above);
  - 8X-4 7's raid split-up note (more sign-ups than `lobbies.raid`), 10's "a spot named exactly beats gyms that contain it" (no such place in North Side), 13's gym deletion, 15, 16 beyond the lines (ring at a spot, route mode, popups: with the UX pass), 23's turn-back case;
  - 8X-5 11 (no short max event on), 12 (the throttle);
  - 8X-6 3's other roles, 4's weather and friendship, 6's other refusals, 9b's dialog (after F3), 9d (a Pokébox needs `/settings pokebattler-id`), 9e's raid case, 10's shadow, mega and T3 addresses;
  - 8X-F 9's raid moved to a gym ending in a period and the research/Rocket take-back at such a stop without an address (no such place in North Side; unit tests cover both);
  - the dated checks of 2026-10-24 and 2026-11-14/15.

## From Phase 8X-F (fixes from the 8X staging run, 2026-10-22)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-22-phase-8xf-staging-fixes.md`, tasks 952fc50b..36f1fcf2, each equal to its prototype commit; the brief read by a fresh Opus reviewer before any code: three Important, folded in as R1 to R11; prototype review by two Opus reviewers: one Important (a Dynamax battle's boss listed only as Gigantamax refused as a change of kind) and eleven Minor, then two fix reviews (two Minor), all folded in; typescript-migration merged in with 8R-3, 4c269e98, `endSentence` carried to 8R-3's took-back words; whole-branch review on Opus, high: one Important, handed to 8R-4 (below), and five Minor, fixed in 737aa3b4). Check (8,887 tests), build and database suite (573) green. **Migration 029** (table `MaxEvent`); **a command change** (`/boss tier:`): deploy the commands before staging. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-22-phase-8xf-staging.md` (the nine findings re-checked, then the 8X steps the run of 2026-10-22 did not reach).

- **Claude's calls (Matt may object):** `/boss tier:` rather than a refusal for a boss on no list; in a Gigantamax battle a Pokémon that can Gigantamax is taken as G without asking; the dialogs' per-open token (`~` and the opening moment in base 36) on Meet…, the which-group dialog and Group…, rather than the group's time; `/max spot:` empty lists only the region's power spots; the `MaxEvent` table rewritten whole after each read (no unique key); `endSentence` also ends at `!`, `?` and a masked link; on the map the answer about a battle already at the spot ends at "at this spot."; no small print in `/counters` about `MOVE_NONE`; the Stop button's label unchanged.
- **For 8R-4 (Matt, 2026-10-22): `/want`'s dialog keeps a stable custom id** (`wantbox:p<dex>:set:<mask>[:<form>]`, `src/commands/notifications/wants.ts` `wantBoxId`, `submitOfId`) while it marks the boxes the member holds (`default: true`): Discord's client shows the last submitted values for an id in place of the marked ones (finding 4 of the 8X run), so a want stopped from a DM or `/unwant` comes back ticked when the member reopens `/want`, and Submit writes it back. The rule and its helpers are in place since 8X-F (`discord-framework.md`, `openToken` / `withoutOpenToken` in `src/discord/dialog.ts`): the id ends in `openToken(now)`, `submitOfId` strips it, a test that each opening has an id of its own and an old id still submits; staging checks that a checkbox group is restored as Meet…'s menu was.
- **Only staging can show:** whether Discord's clients (web, Android, iPhone) stop restoring a stale value once the id changes per opening.

## From the staging run of 8X (max battles, 2026-10-22)

Run on the dev bot from the main checkout (typescript-migration 1ce8098b, with 8R-1, 8R-2 and 8C-K: migrations 025–028 applied at the first start; commands deployed), two accounts (Googer driven by Claude, Tester by Matt on the web), one region (#north-side). The step-by-step notes are in the git-ignored `.superpowers/sdd/8x-staging/progress.md`. Stopped by Matt after the main paths of every 8X plan had run; the findings go to a small fix plan (8X-F), then its staging with the steps below that were not run.

- **What passed** (desktop unless said): 8X-1 steps 1–7 (group headings and lines, three rows of buttons, `lobbies.raid` split-up note, a bad `lobbies` refused at the start, `/help shout`, `/shout group:`); 8X-1B steps 5 (phone), 6–8, 9 (web), 10, 11, 13, 16 and parts of 12 and 14; 8X-2 steps 3, 4, 6 and parts of 7 and 8 (60 max bosses read, the trio under Coming); 8X-3 steps 4, 6–8, 10, 11, 18 and parts of 5, 9, 12–15 (the post, the default ends, the ending-soon dialog, one battle per spot, maps links to spots and pins, the moderators' pin notice and its Delete, blocks at a pin, lobbies of 4 and over the size, Meet… and its DM, `/where`, `/end`, Done, the Dynamax want and its DM); 8X-6 steps 2 and 7 (Uxie's team as on 10-21; `/counters` in a max battle's post); 8X-4 steps 4, 6, 9 and part of 11 (kinds, the panel without a route and its kind menu, `/route add` with spots and gyms marked and ordered, the post's route and the 100-lobby); 8X-5 steps 3–7, 13–16 and parts of 8 and 10 (every new route from the browser's console).
- **Findings for 8X-F:**
  1. **Important:** on a max train, a gym's exact full name typed in `/route add` and sent without picking answers "Several places fit" (with two Power Spots that only contain the words) instead of adding the gym: the strictly-better-fit rule of 8X-4 seems to come after the "several of the train's own kind" refusal. (The autocomplete's order is right.)
  2. `/max spot:` with nothing typed offers no choice; the design wants the region's Power Spots (as `/route add` does on a max train).
  3. `/boss` with a typed boss on no max list in a max battle keeps the old tier silently ("Dynamax Machamp (2★)"); `/max` would ask `tier:`.
  4. Meet…'s menu preselects the last value submitted in the web client (Discord keeps a dialog's value per custom id) instead of the group's time: put the group's start time into the form's custom id.
  5. The max events are lost on a restart: ScrapedDuck had already dropped the 10-19 Max Monday on 10-22, so no featured boss this week (8X-2 step 10's question, answered): keep the max events across restarts.
  6. Words: "There is already a max battle against it at an unlisted power spot" reads as any spot (Matt): name the boss and "this spot"; a place name ending in a period gives "Inc.."; "the max battle at Unlisted power spot" in running text → "at an unlisted power spot"; over the lobby size: "somebody should consider moving to another group" (Matt); 8R-1's want dialog: no ", 1 to 5 stars" on the Dynamax choice (Matt).
  7. Notification DMs: their Stop buttons always last, after all text (Matt; "You won't be told about more … for an hour" next to Stop read as if Stop silenced it for an hour).
  8. Check Pokebattler's answer for a max attacker with one move only (Hydreigon "Bite" in Uxie's team).
  9. The 8X-5 checklist's step 14: `region` is the region's id, not its channel id.
- **Matt's notes for later (not 8X-F unless he says so):** raid and max posts alike: the tier after the boss in a raid's title and heading ("Palkia (5★)"); the top of the post the same for both (drop Types from max posts, or add it to raids and trains); a Catch CP line for max battles (research first: whether weather boost applies; Matt's max catches were always level 20). Forever Friends (2025): only Remote Trades and XP are documented, no damage bonus above Best Friends; Pokebattler takes `FRIENDSHIP_LEVEL_5` but answers as for Best Friends: nothing to add.
- **Not run, for the staging after 8X-F:** 8X-1B 9 (Android/iPhone preselection), 12, 13 (phone), 15, 17–24; 8X-2 5, 9; 8X-3 16, 17, 17a/b, 19 (history), and the second report within the hour of step 18; 8X-4 5, 7, 8, 10 (raid train), 12–24 (with 8B-3's leftovers); 8X-6 3–6, 8–10, 9a–e (8B-2's leftovers); 8X-5 8 (a far pin from the map), 11, 12. **Dated:** 2026-10-24 (Lake Trio Max Battle Day: were Dynamax battles up before 14:00, Q-B; do the trio linger in Pokebattler's feed after 17:00, 8X-2 step 11, 8X-3 step 20); 2026-11-14/15 (Wild Area: Gigantamax first, their default ends, 8X-3 step 21).

## From Phase 8X-5 (max battles on the map, the server side, 2026-10-22)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-22-phase-8x5-map-server.md`, tasks 8dbd922e..838061d3, each equal to its prototype commit; the brief read by a fresh Opus reviewer before any code: five Important, folded in as R1 to R13; prototype review by two Opus reviewers: twelve Minor, then two fix reviews (two Minor), all folded in; whole-branch review on Opus, high: one Important (the refusal for another boss at a spot showed the battle of a forum the member is kept out of) and four Minor (the ending-soon question asked with a full window, an unknown boss in Discord words on the map, a blocked member making a pin battle in a race shared with `/max`, docs), fixed in ab6974ae; typescript-migration merged in, ea874c22). Check (8,503 tests), build and database suite (520) green. Not yet staged: the checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-22-phase-8x5-staging.md` (the routes from the browser's console, as the page draws none of it yet). Matt's scope (2026-10-22): the server alone, what the page would use unchanged however it changes after the map's staging (from a CSS polish with layers to choose to more; the technology matters little); the page (`src/web/client/`) gets no feature, only the fields its test fixtures need. No migration, no party state change, no command change (`/max` keeps its words; its report is `reportMaxAs`, shared with the map). `web.md`, "Max battles on the map".

- **For the web UX pass (the page's part of design 7, left out on purpose):** power spots as a layer of their own from zoom 15 (`GET …/api/powerspots`, as "pick a place" mode draws the PokéStops), each with a popup: its name, "Report max battle here" (the form over `GET …/api/max-options`: the bosses grouped by tier in the list's order, the featured marked; `tier` for a boss typed that is on no list; the end, optional; the region when the answer names several) and, for a train's route editor, "Add to route" (the add body takes the spot's key, `w<id>`, already); the ending-soon answer (422 with `soon.choices`) as a question in the form, the report sent again with the choice picked (`soon: { value, end }`); max battles drawn at their spot (`PartiesView.maxes`, `MapMax`: the boss's picture in a ring of a max colour, a new `--max` in `theme.css`; a pin battle at its point) with a popup as a raid's (counts, buttons with Done, the post); Remote on every popup where `attendance.remote.offered` (`POST …/parties/<id>/remote`); the kind on the start form (`kind`) and the panel's kind menu (`MapTrain.trainKind`, `trainKindWords`, `may.kind`, `POST …/trains/<id>/move/kind`, also for a train without a route, whose `panel` is null). A train's line through power spot stops works already (8X-4).
- **Claude's calls (Matt may object):** the map's report takes pins (a point near no power spot), blocked members refused a new battle there and the moderators told, as from `/max`; the ending-soon question is a 422 answer and a second request, no state kept between them (decided anew; an end no longer offered is asked again); Remote is offered on the map for every kind whose post has it; `maxes` is a list of its own, not a fourth kind inside `raids`; a max battle's map fields mirror a raid's names (`tier` the words, `level` the code), also in the pick list (`{ value, name, level, tier, featured, image? }`), where the brief said `tier` and `tierWords`; the train's kind is `trainKind` / `trainKindWords` on `MapTrain` (`kind` says that it is a train); `TRAIN_KIND_WORDS` moved to `src/domain/web/party-data.ts` (the pure view and a page take it; `render/train-words.ts` gives it on); `PowerSpotsView` and the other max types are in `src/domain/web/max-data.ts`, not beside `StopsView`; the moderators' notice of a pin battle reported on the map does not say "on the map" (a meetup request's post does).
- **Remote on meetups** (Matt, 2026-10-22): it feels odd, but stays for now (meetups served PvP tournaments, played fully remote); Matt may change his mind before cutover: one rule, `remoteAllowed`, decides the post's button and the map's press.
- **`/max` changes in one point (F4):** a blocked member whose report would join a pin battle that ends meanwhile is now refused (`MAX_PIN_BLOCKED`, the use given back) instead of making a new pin battle.
- **Only staging can show:** the routes against a real map page once the UX pass draws them.

- **For 8X-4:** a max train's kind gives `/counters` in its post the max tier.
- **Only staging can show:** every role's real answer (only 2/0/1, 3/0/0, 1/0/2, 1/2/0, 1/1/1 and 2/1/0/0 are recorded), the role pages on Pokebattler's site, the new raid link for a shadow raid, a mega and a non-default tier.

## From Phase 8X-3 (the max battle, 2026-10-21)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-21-phase-8x3-max-battle.md`, tasks 49439204..5aac80a8, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review by two Opus reviewers: five Important (a max battle reported to the moderators threw, `/max` could answer twice at the 5-minute mark, the join at a spot decided outside the store, the one-DM hour shared by Dynamax and Gigantamax, the dialog second-guessing a typed end) and fifteen Minor, then a fix review (four Minor) and Matt's two answers, all folded in; whole-branch review on Opus, high, in two parts: one Important (a max battle group's meeting time was never DMed) and fifteen Minor, then a fix review (seven Minor and two open questions), fixed in ac742cff..6c80c37e). Check (8,333 tests), build and database suite (516) green; **migration 025** (`PartyHistory.wayspotId`); **party state version 13**; commands change (`/max` new; `/boss`, `/end`, `/meet`, `/where`, `/group`, `/status` and the sign-up commands' words): deploy the commands before staging. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-21-phase-8x3-staging.md`.

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-21):** max battles always on (`maxBattles` has defaults for the whole block; no off switch); the default end is the next rotation boundary (rotations align to the hour and half-hour; a reported battle is already in progress): the next :00 or :30 in a short event, the next full hour in an all-day event and for a Gigantamax battle, else 21:00, never past it; a report under 5 minutes from its default end asks in a radio dialog ("Until the next rotation" first and picked, or the end about to pass; a plain confirmation when no later rotation; never for a typed `end:`); a Dynamax battle reported before a max event later that day ends at the event's start (**provisional: ask Matt after the 2026-10-24 Lake Trio day whether Dynamax battles were up before 14:00**); the global Wild Area, GO Tour and GO Fest events (in-person city editions out of scope) list the Gigantamax bosses first in `/max`; `/max` reports only a battle that is up now (planning ahead is a Gigantamax train, 8X-4, which gets `/train start:`, design 6.1); a pin ("Unlisted power spot") battle is posted at once, a member blocked by meetup safety cannot make a new one at a pin, and the moderators get a notice of each pin battle (Delete, Go to post; edited when it ends or is closed).
- **Claude's calls (Matt may object):** a blocked member may still join an existing pin battle; the one-DM hour per member, kind and boss is in memory (lost on a restart); "ending soon" means under 5 minutes; a join to a battle that ended while waiting reports once more, then says "The max battle there has just ended"; `/boss` in a max battle prefers the battle's own kind; a forum without room for every tag (Discord allows 20) gets what fits and a warning, and `/admin setup-forums` names what does not fit.
- **Follow-ups:** the same ending-soon question for `/raid` (typed reports only, about 150 lines; a screenshot report would need a buttons question); an ended upcoming boss is offered again after a restart (ScrapedDuck drops ended events; staging step 20 decides whether to keep them); the GO Fest event type (`pokemon-go-fest`) is a guess; a max battle on the map (8X-5); spot targets, catch CP and moves for max bosses (design 13).
- **For 8X-4:** `/train start:` and `kind:` (design 6.1); the state document's next version is 14; migration 026 for `PartyHistoryStop.wayspotId` (check typescript-migration's and `wayfarer`'s newest first).
- **Only staging can show:** the dialog's preselection on Android and iPhone; the rotation and default ends on a real Max Monday, the 10-24 Max Battle Day and the 11-14 Wild Area; Pokebattler's upcoming bosses after their event; the post with five groups on a phone.

## From Phase 8X-2 (max bosses, 2026-10-21, built while Matt slept)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-21-phase-8x2-max-bosses.md`, tasks b8fed546..792b24de, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: one Important (the week's featured boss lost once ScrapedDuck drops its Monday), eight Minor, then two fix rounds (three Minor, one doc comment), all folded in; final review on Opus, high: ready, three Minor fixed in 8844e60b). Check (8,187 tests), build and database suite (515) green; no migration; **`GAME_DATA_VERSION` 6** (the saved game data is downloaded again once at the first start; a failed download then refuses the start); `/admin boss` gains the max tiers and `sync max:`: deploy the commands before staging. Feeds recorded 2026-10-20 with Matt's go-ahead (Pokebattler's `/raids`, ScrapedDuck's events). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-21-phase-8x2-staging.md`.

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-20 night):** every 1–4★ Dynamax boss of the feed is offered (the feed lists the whole pool, unverified); no Gigantamax boss is live now (an empty `RAID_LEVEL_6_MAX` is right); a max event's hours always come from ScrapedDuck, an event without hours whose id has a date takes the clock hours of the nearest event of its kind; a Max Monday's boss is featured all week (until the next Max Monday), a Max Battle Day's or Weekend's during its hours, ahead of the week's.
- **Rulings for Matt to look at (ledger "For Matt"):** a Gigantamax boss an event names is offered as featured during its hours when the game master lists it as Gigantamax, even when Pokebattler does not (Q3); max boss changes are not announced (Q1: a follow-up if wanted); `data/settings.json` gets `maxBattles` in 8X-3, with `/max` (Q2; instances converted before then stay off until their admin adds the block).
- **The live game master:** the 2026-10-10 one lacks Uxie, Mesprit and Azelf as Dynamax (no `breadOverrides`), Eternatus has only `BREAD_SPECIAL_MODE` (left out): staging step 2 checks the live one; off the feed such bosses draw a warning in `/admin boss` and, in 8X-3, cannot be typed.
- **For 8X-3:** widen `AppContext.raidEvents` to reach `maxEvents()`; `currentMaxBossChoices(deps, now)` and `typedMaxBoss` are ready; if Pokebattler keeps upcoming bosses listed after their event (no `endDate` in any max tier; staging step 11), drop an upcoming boss once a featured event naming it has ended; design Q9 (a max battle's default end by the event: 30 minutes in a short event, 60 all day) reads `maxEventAt`'s `short`.
- **Matt's answers (2026-10-21 morning):** rulings 1–5 above taken (the split picker for now: the splitting group is often the smaller, faster one, but size is the only guess there is; staging decides). **Max battles always on (for 8X-3):** trains and meetups cannot be switched off and neither can `raidBosses` (the design's "as `raidBosses` does" was wrong: it is a required block), so Matt asked why max battles would be optional. Recommendation for 8X-3: `maxBattles` gets defaults for the whole block (as `lobbies`), so every instance, old settings files and converted ones included, has max battles on; no off switch unless Matt wants one. **Gigantamax all day at a Wild Area event (for 8X-3, Q9):** ScrapedDuck's `wild-area` events ("Pokémon GO Wild Area 2026: Global", 2026-11-14 10:00 to 11-15 18:00, two days) bring Gigantamax battles nearly all day, sometimes several bosses at once (Matt; G-Max Duraludon announced so far); 8X-2 reads only `max-mondays` and `max-battles`, and the name of a Wild Area event names no boss. 8X-3 should treat a `wild-area` event (and other kinds that bring Gigantamax, if any) as an all-day event for a Gigantamax battle's default end, its bosses coming from Pokebattler's `RAID_LEVEL_6_MAX` or typed. **The other big events (Matt, same morning):** the regional GO Tour (ScrapedDuck `pokemon-go-tour`, late February) and GO Fest Global (late June to July; its ScrapedDuck type is not in today's feed, probably `pokemon-go-fest`: check when it appears) are like Wild Area: Gigantamax battles nearly all day, for max battles only (raids act as usual during them). The feed also lists the in-person city editions of Wild Area and Tour (Sendai, Mexico City, Kaohsiung, Los Angeles), with UTC times (`Z`); the global edition has local times and `global` in its id. 8X-3 should read only the global editions (an in-person one is in another city), and recognise the kinds by a list of event types.
- **Only staging can show:** the real feed's read and its 304s, the cache's version 6 on the first start, how long ScrapedDuck keeps a past Monday (the week's boss after a restart is kept in memory only: persist the max events if it drops the Monday within the week).

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-20-phase-8x1-lobby-sizes.md`, tasks 6ca28244..c8c33d27, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: one Important, six Minor, then one Minor, all folded in; final review on Opus: ready after fixes, one Important (a design seam, below) and eight Minor, fixed in 298cc585 and the plan's documents, re-reviewed). - **Rulings for Matt to look at:** an Interested newcomer is placed by their heads as if they come; a member bigger than a lobby goes to the group with most room and never opens one; the over-size sentence is a line under the group's heading (the heading stays one line beside Join on a phone); `/shout group:` is refused in a party of one group; a shout cut short says how many it pinged, and a moved one with nobody pinged gives its use back.
- **Decided by Matt (2026-10-20), plan 8X-1B before 8X-2:** one plain Join in the bottom row of every post of several groups, the letter buttons gone; with several groups Join asks in a dialog which group, "Any group with room" first where the party has a lobby size (design, section 4, "One Join, below the post"). Also `/group new`'s help ("Later sign-ups land in the newest group") is no longer true there: reword it. Stage on a phone: a heading like `**Group A: label** · 3/4 coming · 1 interested · meeting at …` beside Join.

## From Phase 8M-5M (meetups from the map, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8m5m-meetups-from-the-map.md`, tasks a68582ce..c7cf2e3f, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: one Important, seven Minor, all folded in; final review on Opus: ready after fixes, one Important and three Minor, fixed in 909febbe..cb11516a, re-reviewed). Check, build and database suite green. One additive migration, **021** `MeetupRequest.source` (renumbered from 020 at the merge: 8T took 020). No command change (`/meetup`, `/name`, `/info`, `/meet`, `/cancel-meet`, `/end` behave as before). Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase-8m5m-staging.md` (from `typescript-migration` only).

- **Matt's rulings (2026-10-17):** a meetup's place is a gym, a PokéStop or a pin, never a power spot or another Wayspot (the general rule for `/meetup` was built by the 8S session, d293e74d); the map offers only those and refuses other kinds.
- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away):** making goes only through `MeetupSafety.make`, `/meetup`'s checks first (free), then `MEETUP_THROTTLE` shared with `/meetup`; a blocked member is pointed to `/meetup` (no Appeal on the map); the moderators' post says "on the map"; the maker and staff change a meetup on the map (a start also needs a sign-up, as `/meet`: staff not signed up cannot change the start); no end time when making (set it in the editor); PokéStops are shown only while picking a place (from the view's box, at most 300), not from a zoom level up; meetups are drawn as a ring at their place, above the markers, and listed in the side panel; a new meetup's region follows the place picked unless chosen; a refused change keeps the member's text, drawn from the meetup as it is now, so a second Set applies it.
- **Only staging can show:** the ring above a raid's picture, pick mode (stops loading as the map rests, "Zoom in" said once, clicks on gyms, stops and the map), the editor across answers, the moderators' post "on the map", a held and an approved meetup from the map.
- **Left open (Minor, from the re-review):** information stored with `\r\n` makes an untouched textarea count as typed (it then keeps its text and its own drawn value; harmless) (closed by 8M-6).
- **Next for this work (Matt, 2026-10-17):** a small plan for the small things left open by the web-map plans (8M-1 to 8M-5M); at staging, expect revisions to make the map more beautiful (functional but plain now). Then the userscript's work (kept with the Wayspot export tool).

Closes the small "Left open" items of 8M-1, 8M-2, 8M-3, 8M-4, 8M-4S and 8M-5M (marked in their sections).

- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away, 2026-10-17):** a raid report is refused when the member is kept out of the forum it would go to or join (`reportBarred`, one check for `/raid`, the map and screenshot reports; this also refuses every `/raid` in a region whose own raid forum denies the member, which `/raid` allowed before), in one sentence ("You cannot report a raid at this gym: you are kept out of the forum its raid would be in."; the map's 403 no longer says "Not allowed."); the region editor's save is stale only for this server's regions and the channels the save uses; a refused channel name in the editor takes off the channel staged before it; a member without `settings.web.memberRole` is told the role's name on the map (not on the editor, never to someone outside the server); every raid on the map carries its place; the page's look is named values in `src/web/client/theme.css` (Leaflet reads its colours from them; `page-theme.test.ts` refuses a colour elsewhere), nothing restyled.

The editor's sentences naming colours ("shown in red", "(orange)", "dashed blue") are prose and need changing by hand if those colours change.

(closed by 8W-3: the check at the join, the retry, an isolated test of Leaflet's routing)
