## From Phase 8S-2 (reports to the moderators, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8s2-moderation-reports.md`, tasks bc7d488e..5ce23906, each equal to its prototype commit; design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-moderation-reports-design.md`, brainstormed with Matt; prototype review by three Opus reviewers (A1–A10, B1–B11, C1–C11: nine Important), two fix rounds, the first re-reviewed (F1–F7, Minor); whole-branch review (Opus, high): ready after fixes, W1–W8 fixed in 81e11150..36eca7cd; merged 604898af). Check 7,758, build, database suite 470 green. No new package. Migration 022 (`2026_10_17_022_moderation_reports`: `ModerationReport`, `ModerationReportReason`, `MeetupPlaceAsk`, the ruling's name, place and source on `MeetupPlaceRuling`, `MeetupBlock.memberName`; additive). Party state stays at version 11. Conventions: `docs/architecture/moderation.md`, `meetup-safety.md`; message commands in `discord-framework.md`.

- **Matt's decisions (2026-10-17):** a member's `/delete` becomes `/report` (every party kind) and "Report to moderators" in the Apps menu (a party's post, copy or link line: the party; any other message: that message), private to the moderators' channel when it is set, the public line and ping as before when it is not; no buttons on posts; `/delete` staff only; closing from a report or `/delete` asks archive or delete; raids and trains never lead to a ruling; a reported meetup's place refused is a lasting ruling that may be asked about; Ask the moderators after 14 days per place; `/places` a command of its own; `/delete` and `/places` hidden by Manage Messages (a server can change it under Integrations); no `/abuse`.
- **Calls for Matt to look at:** the plan's "Decisions in this plan" (limits, form wording, the old `/delete` form taken as a report, a raid's anonymous reporter named in the staff-only post, the reported meetup ended last on Block maker, reopen on a failed step, claim-first Delete message, unarchive and re-archive as it was, ten entries a page). The fix wave also: a reported message keeps its first copy, marked when edited since; a forward is copied from its snapshot; a report of a party closed by staff `/delete` reads closed by that moderator. The members' announcement draft (`2026-10-16-phase-8l2-announcements.md`) lost its sentence about Join buttons for several groups to make room for `/report` (1,976 characters with one map line): Matt may want to check that cut.
- **Not yet on Discord:** the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase-8s2-staging.md` (40 steps; it starts with migration 022 and `npm run deploy-commands -- --global`: `/report`, `/places`, the Apps-menu entry and `/delete`'s permission).
- **Accepted:** reasons carried over from a failed or swept report are kept in memory and lost on a restart; a report whose party ended by itself just before a moderator chose Delete post reads "Ended" and keeps a Go to post link to the deleted post.
- **Left open:** withdrawing a pending request or a report; whether the party history records that a party was reported or closed by the moderators; a setting for the 14 days; `history-recording.db.test.ts` (about line 640) still sleeps and counts lock waits once, where `test/support/lock-waits.ts` would do; `cutover-check` does not look at channels outside regions for Manage Messages (message reports can come from anywhere).
- **The web branch:** the map's refusal of a place that may be asked about points to `/meetup` (`src/web/meetup-routes.ts`); the wayfarer session was told, and its 8W-3 takes 023 if it needs a migration.
- **Only staging can show:** the Apps menu on a phone, the archive-or-delete question, Delete message in an archived and locked post, the moderators' posts within Discord's limits, the asks' DMs.

## From the staging run of 8T and 8T-F (2026-10-17 evening, two accounts)

Run on `typescript-migration` at 12fc17b6 (bot logs `var/stage/logs/bot-8t-1.log`, `bot-8t-2.log`; notes in the git-ignored `var/stage/results-8t.md`; database checks with `var/stage/q-8t.mjs`). Ash was Matt's Googer account, Dawn the Tester account; there was no third account.

- **Passed:** steps 1–11 (step 11 without Iris's ping), 16, 19, 21–28. The confirmation and its texts, Cancel, the split with both posts' small print and thread lines and no new-train DM, the refusal for a group that is the whole train, the anonymous presser, a finished route split (Route complete, Previous back to the stop it finished from), C after B, the refusals in a raid's and a meetup's post, region copies without Move train… and Split group…, both trains on the map (a join from the map after a Leave), two splits listed newest first, a restart (nothing pending, version 11 read back), the history rows (`splitFromId`, `splitOff`, `leftAt` and `signedUpAt` at the split, no steps left), `/help`, the phone.
- **Not run (need a third account; Matt may have one later):** steps 12–15, 17, 18, and the ping part of step 11.
- **Second run, still two accounts (2026-10-18 evening, `typescript-migration` 17735332, `bot-8t-3.log`, notes in `var/stage/results-8t-3.md`), Googer standing in for Iris where a staff account may:** all pass.
  - 11 with 8T-G: an Anonymous member alone in B splits it off. The confirmation says "it has no conductor"; the new post has no "Conductor:" line and no "Created by".
  - 12–13: the conductor, mid-route at stop 3, splits a group they are not in. The menu offers A and B; for B it says "it has no conductor; you stay the conductor of …". The new train is at stop 3 with no conductor. The moved member is pinged with `/settings mentions` on, and not with it off.
  - 15, in part: `/group split`'s autocomplete offers a member their own group only, and staff every group with somebody in it. The command's confirmation matches the button's.
  - 17: the presser relabels the group, since `/group label` names only one's own. The old confirmation is redrawn with "The groups of this train changed meanwhile.".
  - 18: Discord's client greys a pressed button out, so a double press on one confirmation reaches the bot once. Two confirmations of one member, pressed one after the other, both reached the bot: one train, both answered "Split off: <link>".
  - **Decided (Matt, 2026-10-18), for a small plan 8T-H:** the split's confirmation becomes a Discord dialog (modal) in place of the private reply ("it also avoids having to scroll to find the reply to answer it"). The dialog's title is "Split a group off the train". Its text says what becomes of the group, as the reply does now. For the conductor and staff it has a group picker, whose options carry each group's detail, for example "Group B (Fast) · 2 trainers · it has no conductor", since a dialog's text cannot follow the pick. Its buttons are Discord's fixed Submit and Cancel. After Submit comes a short private answer, "Split off: <link>" or the reason. A group changed while the dialog was open is refused with "The groups of this train changed meanwhile: press Split group… again." in place of today's redraw. `/group split` opens the same dialog. One dialog is open per person, which settles the question of several confirmations (step 18's second variant). Mockup first; it replaces the confirmation half of 8T's approved mockups (`split-v1.html` section 2), and staging steps 5–7, 12, 17 and 18 change with it.
  - **Decided (Matt, 2026-10-18): after 8T-H, a small plan turns the other one-shot "are you sure?" replies into dialogs.** These are the good candidates of a sweep of the 28 private replies with controls; each is quick, can open within Discord's 3 seconds, and does not follow a form:
    - Remove all… under `/wants` and `/targets`, with `/unwant-all` and `/untarget-all` (`commands/notifications/remove-all.ts`);
    - staff's `/delete` (`render/moderation.ts` `removalQuestion`, which gains a Cancel);
    - Close…, Refuse place… and Block maker… on the moderators' report post (`components/moderation-reports.ts`, the same question);
    - Delete… on `/gym show` and `/gym delete` (`commands/gyms/delete.ts`);
    - `/region remove` (`commands/regions/remove.ts`, the forum's fate as a radio choice);
    - `/location` moving a raid to another region (`commands/raids/location.ts`).
  - **Left as private replies, from the same sweep:**
    - Too slow to answer within 3 seconds, or following a form: the `/gym import` and `/region import` previews, `/admin cleanup-legacy`, `/raid` after a screenshot, and the `/gym move` and `/gym add` questions.
    - Panels used again and again: Move train, `/gym show`, `/gym review`, the `/wants` and `/targets` lists, `/help`, `/places` and `/iam`'s menus.
    - Results with pictures: screenshot feedback and `/rank`.
    - Possible later: the `/target` picker, a meetup refusal's Ask or Appeal in front of its form, and `/gym add` with its details in one form.
  - **Still not run (a third account):** step 14, Iris refused while the conductor is in her group and somebody else in A; step 15's staff split of a train they are not on, which needs two filled groups; Iris's ping in step 11.
- **Checklist faults:** step 20 cannot be done as written: `/delete` archives and locks the post, and Discord disables the controls of every message in a locked thread, the private confirmation's too, so nothing can be split off an ended train (the right outcome); "This train is over." only answers a press in the moment between the end and the lock (unit-tested). Step 26 must use a pair whose group never signed up to the old train again: a new sign-up replaces the member's split-off row (step 20 did that on Saturday Loop; Two Regions was checked instead). Step 1's deploy needs the stage settings (`set SETTINGS_PATH=var/stage/settings.json&& npm run deploy-commands -- --global`): without them the deploy reads `data/settings.json`, which has no `web`, and leaves `/maps` and the other web commands off the dev application (it happened in this run; Matt redeployed, 65 commands).
- **Decided and built (Matt, 2026-10-17: "b is the behavior we want"; branch `split-anon`):** an Anonymous presser who is in the group no longer becomes the new train's conductor (it named them, "Conductor: Tester", although the post names no maker): the new train has none, as a `/train` made anonymously, until `/conductor set`, and anyone signed up may move it; the old conductor who goes with the group still leads it. The confirmation says "it has no conductor" (it reads the privacy). Staging step 11 checks it.
- **Seen, no fault:** the region channel posts "New train: <name> (B)" for a split-off train, as for any new train (the spec leaves out only the new-train DMs); in the web client a "Split off from" link to an archived post reads "#unknown" after a reload (the phone shows the title).

## From Phase 8W-3 (the Wayfarer small fixes, 2026-10-17)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-17-phase-8w3-wayfarer-small-fixes.md`, tasks f8c53b91..52db5827, each equal to its prototype commit; prototype review: two Important, six Minor, folded in and re-reviewed; final review on Opus: ready after fixes, one Important (a refused geocode held back the whole Google line) and eight Minor, fixed in 72cc8605..0e44fcfe, re-reviewed ready). Check (7,808), build and database suite (478) green. No new package, no migration. Not yet on Discord: the staging checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase-8w3-staging.md`. Closes the "Left open" items of 8W-1, 8W-1R, 8W-2 and 8M-6 (marked in their sections).

- **Rulings for Matt to look at (made while Matt was away, 2026-10-17):** a Wayspot a newer scan no longer had is kept as a row of kind `gone` in `Wayspot` (its former kind in `formerKind`; `all()` skips it; nothing prunes these rows), so an older export cannot bring it back, no migration; of two gyms holding one Wayfarer id the nearer keeps it and the other is asked about ("shares its Wayspot … with …"); a typo'd search ranks by words found, then by the nearer correction read from the name, nickname and keywords only (a word found only elsewhere costs 3), and a stop or power spot word that looks misspelt (one letter off in at least 3 titles and 3× as many) also offers its corrections (thresholds guessed); "(no address available)" only beside a place's picture (also in 8S-2's moderators' views, which show none); a report that joins a raid in another region's forum asks Discord at the join, and a raid whose forum changed meanwhile is refused (a refusal at the join counts as a use of `/raid`); a screenshot's gym question met with Discord away stays answerable with a fresh question time (its text cut at 1,800); while only Geocoding is refused, the Google line goes on with the gyms that want their places alone.
- **Only staging can show:** a real export imported, and an older one after it; the misspelling thresholds and the search's speed on the Pittsburgh export (step 10); `/gym review`'s waiting and sharing sentences; a screenshot's question retried.
- **Left open (Minor):** the review's gathered #bot-updates summary is lost on a crash (needs a migration; documented); a places-only gym put in the Google line while the line waits out a geocode refusal waits with it (as before this plan); the check at the join runs outside `PartyStore.update` (a raid whose forum changes in between is refused; the question itself is async); gone rows are never pruned and every preview reads them all.
- **Next for this work:** the staging of everything since 8W-1R that needs Matt.

## From the staging run of 8S-2 (2026-10-18, two accounts)

Run on `typescript-migration` at 9e4e127f (migration 022 was already on the dev database; global deploy with the stage settings; bot logs `var/stage/logs/bot-8s2-1.log` to `bot-8s2-4.log`; a read-only thread-flags tool `var/stage/thread.mjs`). Googer (owner, staff) reported and decided; Tester was the member, maker and author. Rulings were aged with `var/stage/q.mjs` UPDATEs.

- **Passed:** steps 3, 5–9, 11–16, 17 (a press after a restart), 19–28, 32–34, 36, 37. Among them: the forms and "Sent to the moderators."; reasons gathering on one report (also from the post, the link line and the Apps menu); "Nothing to report there." on a moderators' post; the throttle's sixth report; Close…, Refuse place…, Block maker… with Archive and Delete, their answers and report lines; a report ending with its party on the clock, by staff `/delete` archive and delete; `/delete`'s question asked before anything is locked; message reports with the attachment's name, "in the post of …", the first copy kept after an edit, a forward, Delete message, "Gone" for a message deleted first, the refusals without Manage Messages and without Manage Threads (the archived post never opened), Delete message in an archived and locked post re-archiving and re-locking it; the place's ruling "from a report" in `/places`; the ask with its button after 14 days, "with the moderators already", Keep refused, a second ask after `keptAt`, Allow place; `/places` list, Lift block with an open appeal (one DM), Undo ruling (and its log line); Block member on a refused request; "(gym; officially Frog Fountain)"; without `channels.moderation` the public line and ping, and a message report refused.
- **Run the next morning (2026-10-18, on 610d9a23 with the blockIn fix; logs `bot-8s2-6.log` to `bot-8s2-8.log`), all passed:** 30 (the Ask button of an answer from before a restart opens the form and posts the ask), 29 (an ask moved 8 days back expired on the next tick: its post and the DM), 35 (Undo ruling closes the open ask's post, status `undone`), 18 (a train with a copy in another region: Close… → Delete post removes the post, the copy and the link line, all 404), 10 (Tester let into `#meetup-review` and pressing Dismiss: "Only admins and moderators can do that."), 4 (Tester with a role "Moderator", Manage Messages without Manage Server: `/delete` and `/places` offered, `/admin` not; `/places` works), 38–39 (rehearsal mode, `var/stage/settings-rehearsal-8s2.json`: both reports reach `#meetup-review`; Delete message on a region channel's message refused with the log line), 40 (`cutover-check`: `reports` passes for the test server; its one FAIL is the dev bot lacking Manage Threads in the other server it is in, the second development server, unrelated).
- **Not run:** the second half of 17 (two moderators pressing at once: one moderator account). Step 31 passed after a normal-mode restart (`bot-8s2-9.log`): the web map refused a meetup at a stop refused 15 days back with the pointer to `/meetup` and no button (a first try passed the check only because Tester still had the Moderator role of step 4: staff skip it).
- **Guide fix (Minor):** the moderators' channel must be visible to every staff role (Admin, Moderator), not only "staff-only": a Moderator without View Channel there gets "You don't have access to this link" from every link of `/places` and the reports (seen in step 4). The guides (`new-instance.md`, `migrating-a-legacy-instance.md` 4.x) and `cutover-check`'s `moderation` line could say so.
- **Found (Minor, for a later plan):**
  - "You already reported this." and the report throttle are told only after the form is sent, so the reason typed is lost; both could be checked before the form opens (a quick read).
  - The reported message's line in a party's post repeats the post: "in ⁠Train · Whole Group (B), in the post of Train · Whole Group (B)".
  - Once an ask is decided, its post's Ruling line shows the new ruling ("Allowed … from an ask") instead of the refusal that was asked about.
  - A failed `/meetup` (the post could not be made) still counts against the `/meetup` throttle.
  - Without Manage Threads (Discord shows "Manage Posts" on a forum) the bot cannot create a post in the forum at all ("Missing Access", 50001; probably the forum tag), and the member sees only "Something went wrong (ref …)". `cutover-check`'s `reports` line warns about Manage Threads, but a plain answer naming the missing permission would help.
- **Seen, no fault:** Discord itself refuses a form whose required field is white space alone ("Something went wrong. Try again.", nothing reaches the bot); the web client shows "@unknown-user" beside a member's name after a reload.
- **From the wayfarer + web session (2026-10-18, on d1679b32):** "blocks a member once when two block them at the same moment" (`meetup-safety-repository.db.test.ts`) failed once under the full database suite with a MySQL deadlock in `blockIn` on the `MeetupBlock` insert; alone it passed 5 of 5. `blockIn` may need to retry on `ER_LOCK_DEADLOCK` (or lock in a fixed order); to fix in a small plan. **Fixed 2026-10-18 (branch `fix/blockin-deadlock`):** the test's `fresh()` deletes the block, and InnoDB keeps a deleted row (delete-marked) until its purge; two `INSERT IGNORE`s of that key each lock it shared to see the duplicate, then both want it exclusive to use it again (InnoDB's deadlock report showed exactly that). Production never deletes a block, so it is mostly the tests', but `addBlock` and `blockMaker` now run again on a deadlock (`retryOnDeadlock` in `src/db/transaction.ts`, three tries, once only inside a caller's transaction; `isDeadlock` in `src/db/errors.ts`). A new db test races six blocks over a deleted row for 300 rounds, by turns `addBlock` and `blockMaker`, with a snapshot holding off the purge: it deadlocked on every run before the fix and passed ten runs in a row after. The memory twin and the shared cases need no change.
- **The other transactions reviewed for deadlocks (2026-10-18, Matt):** an Opus review of every `inTransaction` in `src/db/repositories/` found three that deadlock under concurrent use, each now with a db test that deadlocked on every run before its fix: `addReport` (a decided report's key waits for purge; two first reasons after a decision deadlock even under read committed) and `HistoryRepository.optOut` (two members opting out at once, each in the party the other made) run again through `retryOnDeadlock`; `liftWithAppeal` (`/places`) now locks the appeal before the block, in `liftOnAppeal`'s order. `PartyRepository.saveBoth` uses `retryOnDeadlock` too (c9e32e5c). The rest were judged safe: serialised by a lock or queue, admin-only, or without a lock cycle. Not changed, the same delete-marked key shape at lower odds: `addAsk` and `addAppeal` (single statements; a member who meets the error presses again).
- **Flaky under load (2026-10-18, seen once in a full `npm run check` on Windows):** `raid-screenshot.test.ts`, "asks which gym when gyms of one region share the name, offering each told apart": the record kept `pickedGymId: null` instead of 13. It passed 6 runs of 6 alone and the next full check. `ScreenshotStore.change` logs and swallows a failed write (`could not change a kept screenshot`), and `write` replaces the record by `rename`, which Windows can refuse for a moment while another process holds the file; not proven. To look at: whether the test should wait for the change it checks, and whether `picked` should retry the rename (production runs on Linux).
- **Left on the test server:** Temple Sinai Menorah and Frog Fountain without a ruling; Forbes Little Free Library allowed; open message reports in `#meetup-review` ("Edit me", "Archived two", the forwarded one, the one in Whole Group (B)); a refused request "Frog test"; Tester not blocked; the bot's permission entries on `#test-region-east` and test-region-east-meetups back to neutral; `var/stage/settings-nomod.json` (no moderators' channel) beside the stage settings.


## From the staging run of the web map plans (8M-2F to 8M-6, 2026-10-18, Googer and Tester)

Run on typescript-migration d70b62e2 (and its successors with no web change), bot logs var/stage/logs/bot-8m3-1.log .. bot-8m2f-1.log. Claude drove Discord and the map as Googer and, from a /maps link of Matt's, as Tester; permission entries were set through the bot (var/stage/set-overwrite.mjs, Matt's go-ahead) and put back. Matt asked to judge functionality only, not the look.

- **Findings to fix:** Move… on a train's popup closes the popup at once (Important, 8M-5 below; blocks 8M-5 steps 13-15, 17); Cancel in a gym's spawn or raid form closes the whole popup (Minor, 8M-4S); a refused map report writes no log line (Minor, 8M-4); the /maps and /region edit answers should say not to share the login button (Minor, Matt 2026-10-18).
- **Done in 8S-4 (2026-10-19): overflow categories `<name> 2`, see "From Phase 8S-4".** ~~Must-fix before the cutover (Matt, 2026-10-18): `/region import` and full categories.~~ Each new region takes two places in its category, its text channel and its forum, and Discord allows 50 a category (`MAX_CATEGORY_CHANNELS`). `planImport` counts only the 25 new channels of one import (`MAX_NEW_CHANNELS`), not the room left in the category: 25 regions into one category filled it (25 channels, 25 forums), the next import's preview promised the last 3, Discord refused them, and the answer said only `limit` (`notImported: { limit: 3 }`) with no word of the full category. The plan should count forums and the room in each category, and either open another category or say plainly that the category is full (50 channels, forums count) and which areas wait. Worked around on the test server by a second category. (Pittsburgh's real layout, a category per region, would not meet it; a KML whose areas share a description would.)
- **Matt's idea for the region editor (2026-10-18, for its UI pass):** dragging a corner that two (or three) regions share should move it in all of them, so a border between neighbours is edited once instead of region by region; a toggle (on by default) to drag one region's corner apart when that is wanted. Leaflet-Geoman has snapping, not shared vertices: this needs the editor to know which corners coincide (the outlines after Fix all share their corners exactly).
- **Matt's second idea for the region editor (2026-10-18; nice to have, not needed for the cutover: Pittsburgh's real regions have only slivers, the one big overlap seen came from the test squares):** a "Fix overlap" for an overlap that is no sliver (listed today as "It is no sliver between two or three regions: draw it by hand.", e.g. one region drawn over a part of another): "Give it to #A" / "Give it to #B", cutting the area out of the other region, with the places that change region listed as Fix does. Slivers (thin overlaps and gaps along a shared border) are already Fix / Fix all.
- **Region editor, small (Matt, 2026-10-18):** with no overlap and no gap left the list's section disappears altogether; say "No overlaps or gaps." instead, so a clean set of regions reads as clean. ("Fix all slivers…" is off also while the check runs and when only non-sliver overlaps remain, so it is no sign by itself.)
- **Region editor UX (Matt, 2026-10-18):** Fix… and Fix all slivers… draw their panel at the top of the side panel, above the region list, while the map moves to the sliver: with the list scrolled, the choices and Apply are out of sight and the buttons seem to do nothing. The panel should come into view (or open beside the sliver, as a popup or a pinned box). Part of the editor's UI and look pass: Matt finds it functional but poor to use and not pretty yet.
- **8M-6 steps 3-6 passed** (Matt, 2026-10-18, drawing in the editor): the near-name warning, the backslash and dotted names in the preview, an existing channel's name refused with Save off and Undo, a save not refused for a channel the save does not use.
- **Still to stage:** 8M-6 steps 3-6 (the region editor, drawing: Matt); 8M-2F's save (needs real channels) and the Pittsburgh Wayspots; 8M-6 step 14 (the look).

## From the staging run of 8W (8W-1, 8W-1R, 8W-2, 8W-3, 8W-4; 2026-10-18)

Claude drove Discord in Matt's Chrome (Claude in Chrome, tabs of its own; Googer); the export tool 2.0.0-dev and the bot from `typescript-migration` (de97c7f8 built, the bot at 8dfac5ff), stage settings, no migration, no deploy. Full notes in the git-ignored `var/stage/results-8w.md`.

The Wayspot export tool's part of this section is kept with the tool.

**`/gym import` and `/gym review` (8W-1 steps 10, 12, 13; 8W-1R step 14; 8W-2 steps 4-6; 8W-3 step 2 pass).** The first import of the export: preview (5,294 new gyms, 14 to review, 33,256 stops and power spots new), Cancel ("Nothing was imported."), Import: 19:40-19:45, the gyms added one by one (about 60 ms each with their upkeep, under the gym admin lock: this is how long a city's first import holds `/gym`), the Wayspots in one transaction; "Wayspots loaded" 36,309 in 192 ms, the search rebuilt in 577 ms; one post in bot updates ("added 5,294 gyms and renamed 1. It also added 18,206 PokéStops and 15,050 power spots."). The same file again: "would change nothing", no buttons. Two gyms given one Wayfarer id: "Imaginary Bench (#56) shares its Wayspot Orchid Longfellows with Orchid Longfellows (#23), which lies nearer to it: that gym is the Wayspot now.", Delete (its confirmation and Back) / Keep / Skip for now, no Same as (#56 first carried a "not in Wayfarer" mark from an earlier staging, which the import rightly left alone; the mark was cleared in the database for the step).

**Searches (8W-3 steps 8, 10).** `/wild near:libary` in #oakland: "Little Free Libary (PokéStop)" first, then the libraries, no Vicary House. Times from the typed text to the suggestions, Discord's round trip included (the bot logs none with `debug: false`): `libary` 1.08 s, `little free libary` 0.67 s, `st pauls churh park` 0.76 s, `/find-gym libary` 0.42 s. A bot-side timing needs a run with `debug: true`.

The Wayspot export tool's part of this section is kept with the tool.

- **Must-fix before the cutover:** none from this run.
- **Findings (Minor):** "28 of the scan's areass are no region of this server" and "14 of thems are there already" (doubled plurals in the import's preview); the preview's first lines print numbers without thousands separators (41639, 5294, 2639) beside "33,256"; "That file is no KML of regions (not-xml)." shows an internal code; `/find-gym libary` lists, after the real matches, gyms of bloomfield-strip-lawrenceville with nothing like "library" in name or places (Pittsburgh Ballet Theatre, Westinghouse RR Air Brake, Green Energy, Pittsburgh Brewing Company …): to be explained. (The export tool's findings are kept with the tool.)
- **Ruled (Matt, 2026-10-18), for the next Wayfarer plan:** the Google line after a city's first import (5,316 gyms queued, 5,209 for places only, 107 for an address; about 53 days at `googleLookupsPerDay` 100) is fine as it is slow; the line becomes a priority queue: gyms first, then PokéStops, then power spots. Today `GymLookup` holds gyms only (ordered by `queuedAt`, `gymId`) and no Wayspot is asked of Google, so the plan adds the Wayspots to the line (their Google places as search names, like a gym's) with a kind that orders it before `queuedAt`. The budget is counted by month, not by day (Matt): Pine's places question is Nearby Search Pro (field mask `places.displayName,places.types`), 5,000 free a month, then $32 per 1,000; Geocoding (Essentials) 10,000 free a month, $5 per 1,000; the free amounts reset on the 1st at midnight Pacific time (Google's price list, 2026-10-18). Sketch: `googleLookupsPerMonth` (about 4,500) in place of `googleLookupsPerDay`, counted per month and per kind (places and addresses apart; the address ceiling the 10,000), every places question counted against it (also those outside the line: a gym added or moved by an admin), a reserve of about 500 kept for those, the line paced evenly (what is left of the month divided by the days left) for stops and power spots only; gyms may take all that is left of the month at once, the reserve kept (Matt: a later bulk import's gyms then find budget left; Pittsburgh imported on the 1st: 4,500 gyms the first day, the other 816 on the next 1st, then stops at about 125-150 a day). Addresses are no concern: the import takes the Wayspot's address already, and Geocoding is asked only for a gym that has none after it (107 of 5,316), always together with its places. At about 4,500 a month Pittsburgh's gyms take about 5 weeks, its 18,206 stops and 15,050 power spots about 7½ months more. The key's use, 2026-10-12 to 18 (Matt, Cloud console): Places (New) 343, Geocoding 37, Maps Static 34, Places (legacy) 3 (legacy Pine's tests); nothing else draws on it, so about 4,500 with a reserve of 500 is safe.
- **Ideas for the next Wayfarer plan (Matt):** a first city import's gyms in blocks (a few hundred per transaction, the upkeep per block) instead of one by one (for 8W-6). (The ideas for the export tool are kept with the tool.)
- **Not run (for another run, mostly Matt's):** the Tester steps (8W-3 step 11), the map's right click (8W-3 step 13), a channel without Embed Links (8W-2 step 16), the CSV in a spreadsheet (8W-1 step 11, a download), production's gyms (8W-1 step 18, 8W-1R steps 18-19), 8W-4 step 13 and the hidden-tab part of step 7; 8W-1 step 15 (the review queue holds only the old test gyms in no region, where no raid can be), 8W-2 step 15 (the stage settings' `unown-sightings` is not on the test server); 8W-1R steps 9, 11, 13; 8W-2 steps 7, 10; 8W-3 steps 3-7, 9, 12. The scan was over the Google Maps KML, not `/region export` of the test server, so no region counts as scanned and the bulk delete beyond the area could not be offered (8W-1R step 16 needs a scan of `/region export`).
- **Dev database after the run:** 5,346 gyms (Pittsburgh's Wayfarer gyms plus the old test gyms; #23 is now "Orchid Longfellows", #56 "Imaginary Bench" shares its Wayfarer id, kept), 36,309 Wayspots, 14 review items (the old test gyms never found, all skipped once), 5,316 gyms in the Google line.

## From the staging run of 8S-3 (2026-10-18 evening, Googer and Tester; bot-8s3-1 to -3.log)

- **Pass:** steps 1 to 20, apart from what is listed below. West and East were #oakland and #squirrelbreeze. Reports were refused before the form (already reported; the sixth in an hour, by `/report` and the Apps menu alike; six forms cancelled counted nothing). Asks and appeals were refused before the form after three in the hour. A reported message was named "in the post of Train · …" once. A decided ask kept the ruling it asked about (Allow place, Keep refused, Undo ruling on `/places`). `/meetup`, `/raid`, `/train` and `/location` were refused plainly in a forum without Manage Posts, uncounted (two meetups and two trains worked afterwards, the third throttled). The map's raid, meetup and train gave the same sentence, uncounted (two map trains worked afterwards). The held meetup approved in that forum gave the reason to the moderator and in the maker's DM. `/admin setup-forums` with Create Posts denied said "closed it to posts by members" and allowed the bot Send Messages again. cutover-check named the forum's Manage Posts in the region and `reports` lines, and the Moderator role that cannot see #meetup-review. The Move panel, Cancel and Done, and clicks outside and inside a popup all behaved as written. The map's log lines (no-gym, refused, not-here, no-region, refused) held no text, name or point. The login button's small print was right.
- **Checklist fixes (Minor; done in 8S-4, and step 11's ruling built):**
  - Step 12: the bot remakes a missing summary post when it starts (the startup sweep), so "delete it, then run setup-forums" never shows setup-forums' own line. The summary was made without Manage Posts and its pin was refused ("Missing Access", counted as refused); only the line could not be seen. To see it, delete the post while the bot runs and run setup-forums at once.
  - Step 11: the request's post says only "…but the meetup could not be made. Its maker was told."; the reason goes to the moderator who pressed (private answer) and to the maker (DM). **Ruled (Matt, 2026-10-18): the post names the reason too**, for the other moderators (a fix for the next small plan; the post's line in `src/render/meetup-safety.ts`, the failure kept with the decision).
  - Step 17: a raid's Change… has Done, not Cancel.
  - Steps 1 to 3: the names "Meetup · … one" and "… two" were too alike to follow; use distinct names.
- **Finding (Minor, map; done in 8S-4: a train at a gym is a ring around it):** a train's dot at a stop lies over the gym's marker, so the gym's popup and its raids cannot be reached while the train is there (Schenley Fountain, a stop of the train's route). Raise the gym marker on hover, or offer the gym's popup from the train's.
- **Wish (Matt, maybe):** `/maps` could open the map on the region of the channel where it was used; today it shows all regions.
- **Not run:** a screenshot report into a forum without Manage Posts (step 9); `barred`, `throttled` and `blocked` in the map's log lines (step 19: they need Tester on the map and 21 reports); a staff role and a members' role that can both see #meetup-review in one warning (step 14; `cutover-check.test.ts`); Googer's side of step 16.
- **State after the run:**
  - The squirrelbreeze summary was remade as post <id>: its recorded id was cleared in the dev database instead of the post being deleted (Claude deletes no posts). Matt deleted the old "Active raids" post (<id>) afterwards; the new one stays unpinned (the bot pins a summary again only when an edit of it fails), so pin it by hand.
  - The dev database has three places ruled for the run: Schenley Plaza (allowed on its ask), Schenley Plaza Events Tent (refused, kept), Andrew Carnegie (ruling undone).
  - Tester's block is lifted, and the Moderator role is off Tester. Moderator can now see #meetup-review.
  - The forum permissions are as before the run.

## From Phase 8T-H (the split's confirmation as a dialog, 2026-10-18 night, merged while Matt slept)

- **Merged** on Matt's go-ahead ("make sure you go ahead and merge it in … since I'm going to bed"): plan `2026-10-18-phase-8th-split-dialog.md`, tasks 8T-H-1 and 8T-H-2 plus the review fixes 8T-H-3 and 8T-H-4. Tip: check 7,924, build, database suite 487.
- **Calls made without Matt (for him to check):**
  - Answers after Submit are plain text (single sentences), not a components-v2 container.
  - Choice labels and descriptions are raw text (not `plain()`), on the assumption that Discord shows no markdown in choices; staging step 28 checks it with a name holding `*`, `_` and a domain and a train name with markdown.
  - A long choice label ends `, and N more` (as the confirmation's lists do), not `…, and N more` as the design's sentence could be read; the group's own label is cut to 40 characters first.
  - When the old train keeps its conductor, a choice's description leaves out `it has no conductor;` (mockup v4).
- **For staging (the 8T checklist):** step 20, a dialog submitted after its train was `/delete`d: what Discord does, and whether the answer opens the archived post again (`parties-and-posts.md` forbids anything after the archiving; the raid's "Set time" form would share it); step 18's double Submit now needs two accounts.
- **Gantt rows** of 8T-H were added at 8Q's close (its task dates are those of the rebased commits, so they look instantaneous).
- **Changed 2026-10-20 (mockup v6, Matt; branch `split-dialog-v6`):** the dialog that asks which group names everybody in a line per group in its text (`**Group B (Fast)** (4 trainers): …`), each choice is only `Group B (Fast) · 4 trainers` with no description, and who leads is said once for the groups whose new train somebody would lead. This replaces the two calls above on choice labels and descriptions; a conductor the bot has no name for is `a trainer`. Why: in-person trains of 40 to 50 are split into groups of 16 to 18 for 20-person lobbies, where a choice named only 11 of 17; the second line said the same in every choice. Open for staging (step 28): how a long dialog scrolls on a phone, and whether Discord counts a dialog's choices against the text's 4,000 characters (the text can be filled to the limit).
- **Open (found 2026-10-22, from the 8X staging run's finding 4):** Discord's client shows a dialog's last submitted values when the same custom id opens again; 8X-F gave Meet…, Group… and the which-group dialog a per-open token (`openToken`, `src/discord/dialog.ts`), 8R-4 the `/want` dialog. The radio dialogs of 8T-H and 8Q still have stable ids: the split's picker (`party:<id>:splitform`), staff's `/delete` (`pclose:<party>:close`), the report post's Close…, Refuse place…, Block maker… (`mreport:<report>:<action>`) and `/region remove` (`region:<id>:remove`). After a refused Submit (the groups changed, Manage Threads missing) a reopened dialog may show the old pick, against "nothing picked" (the harsher way never by default). A small fix: `openToken` on those ids, stripped at Submit. Both checklists (8T, 8Q) now say what is still to run and check this. **Done** the same day (branch `dialog-open-token`): the split's two dialogs, `removalDialog` (`/delete`, the report post's three) and `/region remove` end in `openToken`, their Submits leave it out, and an id without it still submits; staging still checks it on web, Android and iPhone. Its full check once timed out `src/commands/screenshots/mention.test.ts` ("does not count a screenshot answered by its gym question whose raid the bot may not post", 15 s) under load; the file passes alone in about 10 s, and the rerun passed in full: likely the machine's heavy load (Matt, 2026-10-23); look into it only if it recurs.

## From 8W-P (the export tool private, handed out by the owner's instance; complete on `wayfarer`, 2026-10-20)

- **Pine's own license** (Matt, 2026-10-20): GPL-3.0 is fine; a permissive license maybe later. Before any change, check which files that survive the cutover came from other authors (legacy icons in `pictures/`, texts, logic ported from the legacy bot) and under what terms. `packages/wayspot-core` is MIT already (Copyright (c) 2026 Googer), so that the private tool may bundle it; the tool is not open source (`UNLICENSED`, its grant in its `LICENSE`).
- **The private repository is not pushed** (only on Matt's word, to `Googer/Professor-Pine-Wayspot-Export`). Its `package.json` takes Pine's package by a path beside it (`file:../Professor-Pine-wayfarer/packages/wayspot-core` until `wayfarer` is merged, then `file:../Professor-Pine/packages/wayspot-core`; the repository is `C:\Users\<you>\IdeaProjects\Professor-Pine-Wayspot-Export`, its remote `origin` set, never fetched or pushed). Its own tests and docs keep the real Wayspot data of the stagings (private; Pine's package has invented data since the final review). Before a push, Matt decides on what it carries of his name: the copied plans and docs (commit identities, Windows paths, "Matt, <date>" rulings) and some source comments say "Matt" (the built script and README do not); every commit's author is his git identity.
- **Before Pine's first push**, the history step of the cutover runbook (N8): the migration's older commits hold the tool's code and docs.
- **`./pine`'s folder check** (`check_tool_dir`, run by the commands that start the bot: others may read the tool's folder and the two files it serves) has no shell test, as none of `./pine`'s checks has; it was tried by hand in a container as a plain user.
- **Keys in addresses:** a key is in the tool's address, so a proxy in front of Pine (Cloudflare's logs, a reverse proxy's access log) may record it; Pine itself never logs it. A leaked key is replaced by "Make a new link" or revoked by the owner.
- **The owner's DMs of requests** wait in the in-memory DM queue: a restart loses those not yet sent (the page lists every request).
- **Shared code is an interface:** the modules of `@pine/wayspot-core` the tool imports and the two fixtures (`wayfarer.md`); a change to them is checked against the tool's `npm run check` before it is kept.
- **For staging** (`2026-10-20-phase-8wp-staging.md`): every step needs the dev bot (Discord) and Matt's Tampermonkey; Matt removes his `2.0.0-dev` copy first (it has another namespace and never updates). The private repository's docs name the Pine commit they were copied from (`a7e3d324` on `wayfarer`).
- **Matt's start command changes:** tsx ignores `customConditions`, so the bot starts with `node --env-file=.env --conditions=pine-source --import tsx src/index.ts` (CLAUDE.md, Status); without the flag a run fails while `packages/wayspot-core/dist/` is absent and silently uses a stale one after a build.
- **The final review's audit** (Matt's condition for the plan): Pine's tracked files at the tip say nothing of how the tool works, hold none of its code, and the package names no one but "Googer"; the old plans keep the line "kept with the tool" where they spoke of it, and old `git diff … userscript` command lines still name the former folder. An older checkout's `dist/userscript/` and compiled scan modules are git-ignored leftovers: `dist/` is removed before the next build.

## From 8D-I (the summary post taken over, 2026-10-20)

The Minor of "From the staging run of 8B-1 to 8B-4, 8H, 8M-1, 8M-2 and runbook R1" (a start whose summary record points at a gone post made a second "Active raids" post, which Discord would not pin beside the old one: 30047). Done by the controller without a plan document, failing tests first, on branch `fix-summary-pin` (92e3aa87, review fixes 6adb5154), reviewed on Opus (ready after fixes; S1 to S5 folded in). `PartyService.takeOverSummary`: where the record is gone or empty, the bot's open `Active raids` post in the forum is recorded and edited (the pinned one, else the oldest, pinned with its next edit); a forum that changed meanwhile gets no post; a failed look makes a new post with a warning. No migration, no command change. Not yet seen on Discord.

- **Left open:** an archived post that still holds the forum's pin is not found (`openPosts` lists open posts only): whether Discord keeps an archived thread pinned is not known; if staging shows it does, a 30047 refusal in `pinSummary` could look at archived posts. Other open `Active raids` leftovers beside the one taken over stay open (the sweep archives only ended parties). The look for one runs also for a forum made a moment ago (one guild-wide active-threads request per region at a first `/admin setup-forums`; cheap). A party post a moderator renamed `Active raids` by hand would be taken over (far-fetched; `store.byPost` would rule it out). A moderator who pinned a post of their own instead of the summary gets "could not pin the summary post" with every summary edit, as before this fix.
- **R2: the image could not be built (fixed):** `./pine convert-settings` builds the image first, and its build stage failed: `npm run build` runs `web:build` (`scripts/web/build.ts`, since 8M-1), which neither `.dockerignore` let in nor the Dockerfile copied. Both do now, and `src/ops/docker-image.test.ts` checks that every `scripts/…` file the build runs reaches the build stage (red before the fix); a real `docker build` then had the map's assets in `dist/web/assets/`. The same run found `npm run check` failing on other sessions' scratch files (`prettier --check .` reads neither `.git/info/exclude` nor nested `.gitignore` files): `/.claude/worktrees/` and `/.superpowers/` are in `.prettierignore` now.
- **For the final security audit (Matt, 2026-10-20):** `npm ci` warns that `glob@10.5.0` is deprecated (known vulnerabilities). It comes only through `testcontainers` → `archiver` → `archiver-utils`, a dev dependency: not in the image. Check for a `testcontainers` or `archiver-utils` release without it, or an `overrides` entry.
- **R2 and R3 (pass, 2026-10-20):** the copy taken from the old host over SSH (`pine-20261020.sql`, both settings files unread, `parties/` with 2,442 files as on the old host); the converter kept the remote, Unown and elite channels and the map link, renamed `ex-gym-raids` to `elite-gym-raids`, timezone America/New_York; `var/rehearsal/.env` built by a script that prints no value (`OWNER_ID` Matt, `DISCORD_APP_ID` <production application id>, a new `DB_ROOT_PASSWORD`, no backups, `LEGACY_PARTIES_DIR` the copy); the rehearsal block: allowlist Matt only (an admin of the server, is staff anyway and stays off it so that no want of hers can DM her), `squirrelbreeze`, staff role `Moderator`. The restore renamed 15 lower-case tables (4 servers, 28 regions, 3,213 gyms, 2,057 members); `./pine migrate` ran all 23 migrations. Pitfall: forgetting `export PINE_ENV_FILE` makes `./pine` read the checkout's `.env` and refuse (no `DB_ROOT_PASSWORD`): nothing happens.

## From the sponsored export and the first import's dry run (2026-10-21 and 22)

Matt made a one-time list, from another community map, of the sponsored locations Wayfarer never shows. The files are git-ignored in `var/sponsored-export/`: `sponsored-ruled.json` (140 sponsored: 51 gyms, 89 stops, each with the basis of its ruling), the legacy gyms in no source (`legacy-gyms-gone.md`, 82) and those matched by position only (`legacy-gyms-place-only.md`, with Matt's rulings in `legacy-place-only-rulings.json`, made against Campfire).

- **Cutover step: the first Wayfarer import (Matt, 2026-10-22).** In the runbook since 2026-11-01 (N7); step 2 is `./pine seed-gyms` since 2026-11-02:
  1. Cut over as planned: the legacy database taken over as it is, the new bot starting with legacy's gyms.
  2. Before any import, mark the 14 sponsored legacy gyms "not in Wayfarer" (legacy ids 1424, 1453, 1454, 1455, 1468, 1472, 1531, 1534, 1483 Starbucks; 3066, 3071, 3072 Verizon, 3122 Verizon Wireless; 2996 Bantha Tea Bar), then add the sponsored gyms legacy lacks (the other 37 gyms of `sponsored-ruled.json`) and mark them too. A marked gym the export lacks is left alone and never queued (`matchGyms`, `notInWayfarer`); Wayfarer having it after all clears the mark. How the marks and additions are made (a script, or the review's "Not in Wayfarer" answer) is for the cutover work.
  3. Make a fresh Wayfarer export of every region close to the cutover, not the 2026-10-16 file: what is gone is decided by the scan's date.
  4. `/gym import` it and work through `/gym review`.
  5. Later imports then match nearly every gym by its Niantic id, which every match writes.
- **The dry run's numbers** (legacy production's 3,213 gyms, backup of 2026-10-14, through `matchGyms` against `var/stage/wayfarer/pittsburgh-2026-10-16.json.gz`, 43,057 Wayspots; the 28 regions of the dev database; the 14 sponsored gyms marked; no earlier answers; laptop only): 2,957 gyms considered (2,802 inside a region); **2,796 matched** on their own (329 by id, 2,338 by name, 129 by place; 165 renamed, 109 moved); **147 questions** (75 `match`, 56 `unknown`, 16 `missing`; 138 inside a region); the 14 marked left alone; none not covered; **2,599 gyms added** that legacy never had (legacy got no new gyms since Pine-GymReader broke): Pittsburgh's gym count nearly doubles, worth a line in the cutover announcement. Only 339 legacy gyms carry a Niantic id (2,253 an old numeric id, 621 none), so the first import matches by name and place.
- **Rules that could ask less on the first import (not decided):** of the 15 gyms Matt ruled, 6 renamed or moved ones become questions a rule might have answered: the same name and the only Wayfarer gym of that name within 100 m (Cranberry Public Library 78 m, Not in My Hood Sign 75 m; an equal name counts only within 20 m today), and a similar name within about 15 m (Bryn Mawr Church of Christ for "Brn Mauer Church of God" 14 m, Smithton VFD Social Hall 8 m; any title counts only within 5 m today). A gym whose old place now holds a stop of its own name (Lynch Field) is known as one only by id. Worth it only if the real queue is long.
- **For 8R-1:** seed the sponsored stops from `sponsored-ruled.json`'s `sponsored` stops (89). Sponsored means "on that list, not on Wayfarer", ruled by Matt. The list leaves out Mon Valley, Upper Allegheny, Irwin–Greensburg and Latrobe, and covers 12 more regions in part.

## From the 8R-1 prototype (stops of Pine's own and field research, 2026-10-22)

Prototype on `proto-8r1` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/stop-reports/var/proto-8r1`), eight tasks and the docs; area file `docs/architecture/stop-reports.md`.

- **Watch Leek Duck's research page on 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-21:** whether `https://leekduck.com/research/` lists an event's own tasks while it runs (Into the Wild from 2026-11-09 10:00; Pawniard Community Day 2026-11-21 14:00–17:00). Nothing reads event pages; a task missing from the list is typed in full. If the page keeps the ordinary tasks during an event, 8R-1 needs the event's page or a staff list.
- **Research periods (Matt, 2026-10-22):** a multi-day research event that is no big event (Into the Wild, 10:00 to 20:00 over five days, or one crossing midnight) rotates the tasks at its start and end for good, and inside it the tasks roll over at each local midnight: a period per day, `event:<id>:<date>`. Big events keep their daily windows (event tasks in their hours, the day's research before and after, coming back). Design 4.2 and answer 6 to be updated by the controller.
- **`/want`'s autocomplete** offers the Pokémon, forms, tiers and IVs first and the items and Mega Energy after them, at most five places of a full list (fix rounds 1 and 2, B5); the design's "today's encounter rewards first" is not built.
- **`/settings show`** counts the lines of `/wants`, both tables (fix round 1); one more read for it (6 statements).
- **Research DMs** remember in memory who was told of a task at a stop in its period (as max battles' quiet hour): a restart can repeat one. A report whose stops lie in two regions with posts of their own sends one DM per region channel, each with its post's link; a stop in both regions is named once.
- **A DM's link** draws its channel's post at once (`StopPostService.messageOf`, `Gathered.flushKey`): that channel skips the three seconds of gathering when somebody wants the task.
- **The announcement drafts** (`2026-10-16-phase-8l2-announcements.md`): Pittsburgh's post is at Discord's 2,000 characters and does not name `/research` (a swap for Matt: one line out for it); the official post's first message was already 2,176 characters before 8R-1 (2,348 now): split it or trim before it is posted.
- **The invite's permissions integer** in `docs/guides/new-instance.md` is `2252092140285008` with the optional Pin Messages; existing installs give the bot's role Pin Messages by hand (`cutover-check` warns per region channel).

## From the 8R-2 prototype (Team GO Rocket, 2026-10-22)

Prototype on `proto-8r2` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/stop-reports/var/proto-8r2`), eight tasks and the docs; area file `docs/architecture/stop-reports.md` ("Team GO Rocket").

- **Migration 028** (`2026_10_22_028_rocket`): `RocketLineup` and `GruntMessage`. A Rocket report is a `StopReport` row of the kind `rocket` in the day's period, so a grunt and a hideout at a stop share one row and `decideRocket` decides inside the transaction that locks it (`saveUnless`). The brief's `GruntReport` table is named `GruntMessage`: it holds only the messages (one per region channel), the report is the `StopReport` row.
- **Decided in the prototype (for the review):** a grunt's message is not deleted at shutdown, unlike a spawn report's (its report stands on through a restart; the next start's sweep deletes what is overdue); "Already reported" counts against `/rocket report`'s throttle (a guard refusal would not, but the decision is the transaction's); before any lineups were ever read `/rocket` offers every who without catches; a grunt reported late in the evening stands its half hour past midnight in the day it was made; the hour of a grunt's DMs is per member and grunt type whatever fired it (a type want or a Shadow want); `/want`'s Rocket targets share the rewards' five places in a full list; the lineups' catches come after the raid bosses among the Pokémon of `/want`'s list; `RocketLineupService` repeats `ResearchTaskService`'s refresh and guard rather than sharing a base (a refactor for later if a third list comes).
- **For staging:** the grunt message's gallery of catches (one catch, the decoy's Bellsprout, is a large picture at full width: a thumbnail may read better); the hideout dialog on Android and iPhone; "Already reported" and its link; a grunt and its DM after a restart; the pinned post with research and hideouts spilling; the DM's hour note.
- **`cutover-check`:** nothing new: a grunt's message needs Send Messages and Embed Links in the region channel, which spawn reports need already; the post's Pin Messages is 8R-1's.
- **For 8R-3 (the map):** `ctx.rocket.report` and `channelsAt` are the one way to report; the map's confirm takes the hideout's id as `replacing`; `rocketAt` and the standing reports give the layer its grunts and hideouts.
- **CLAUDE.md** (Matt or the controller): the area index line of `stop-reports.md` names Team GO Rocket (`/rocket`, grunts' messages, the hideouts, Rocket wants), and the status line 8R-2.

Check (8,838 tests), build and database suite (568) green; no migration, no command change, no party state change. Make a costume wantable as a narrower want; **a plain species want still covers its costumes** (Matt), as it covers its forms (Armored Mewtwo, `MEWTWO_A`, is a form with its own entry already). ## From the 8R-3 prototype (reports at stops on the map, the server side, 2026-10-22)

Prototype on `proto-8r3` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/stop-reports/var/proto-8r3`), four tasks and the docs; `web.md`, "Reports at stops on the map (plan 8R-3)". Matt's scope (2026-10-22, as 8X-5): the server alone; the page gets no feature. No migration, no command change (no deploy), no party state change.

- **Built:** `/research report` and `/rocket report` / `remove` as one code path each (`reportResearchAs`, `reportRocketAs`, `takeBackAs`, `stopsByKey`; their answers' words `researchWords` / `rocketWords` with the caller's channel namer; the commands' messages unchanged, inline snapshots guard them); `GET /api/stop-reports` (today's research of the period on, the waiting count, grunts, hideouts, as the member may see them; read anew per request, ETag, 304); `GET /api/research-options` and `/api/rocket-options`; `POST /api/research`, `POST /api/rocket` (the hideout question as a 422 with `asks.replacing`), `POST /api/stop-reports/<id>/remove`. `StopPostService.linkOf` (read only) and `GruntMessageService.messagesOfReports` (one read, `GruntMessageStorage.ofReports`) give the links. `PartyViewers` is now one for `/api/parties` and `/api/stop-reports` (made in `guildRoutes`).
- **Differs from the brief's review (R4):** Leek Duck's pictures are already allowed: `cdn.leekduck.com` is the origin of `POKEMON_ICON_BASE` (`pictureOrigins`), so rewards, items, Mega Energy and catches keep the pictures the reports store, through `allowedPicture`; the CSP is unchanged and there is no question about it for Matt.
- **Decided in the prototype (for the review):** a report shows unless every region it lies in is barred to the member (a region without a forum always shows it), so a stop in two regions shows if either is visible; a report in no region now shows to nobody; research links and hideout links fall back to the post's first message where the post does not hold the task (one counted as "…and N more"); a grunt's link is its message in a region the member sees; `mayTakeBack` is in the view (own or staff by the directory's standing; none while read-only) so a page can draw Take back without asking; staff for taking back on the map is `isMapStaff` (admins, moderators, the owner), as `/research remove`'s `isStaff`; the answers on the map leave out the command's heading ("### Field research reported"); "Already reported" on the map names the reporter by name ("a member" for none); the pick lists' `stale` flags (research by `settings.research.staleAfterHours`, the lineups by `settings.rocket.staleAfterHours`); the research view lists only the reports of the period on now and counts those waiting, as the post; reports and taking back share one limit of 30 a minute per address; the hideout question's 422 carries the hideout as the list shows it.
- **For the web UX pass (the page's part of design 8, left out on purpose):**
  - **The layer:** poll `GET …/api/stop-reports` as `/api/parties` is polled (`Poller`, every 20 s while visible, at once after a report or a take-back; 401/403 stop it), only for a member (`/api/me`), drawn when the JSON changed. `StopReportsView`: `research` (`MapResearch`: `id`, `stop { key, name, address, lat, lon }`, `task`, `text`, `category`, `typed`, `rewards`, `endsAt`, `reporter`, `mayTakeBack`, `link?`), `waiting` (`{ stops, at }`: "The day's research comes back at 5:00 PM (6 stops)"), `event`, `grunts` (`MapGrunt`: `who`, `name`, `catches`, …), `hideouts` (`MapHideout`: `who`, `name`, `catches`, `picture?`, …), `zone`. Research drawn with its first reward's `image`, hideouts with `picture`, grunts with a countdown to `endsAt` (and dropped at it without waiting for the poll); several reports at one stop (research and a Rocket report) as one marker with both in its popup. A colour for each (`theme.css`: `--research`, `--rocket`).
  - **The popups:** the stop's name and address line, Directions; each report's words (task and rewards in short, "one of" where drawn; who and the catches), until when, the reporter, "Open on Discord" (`link`, only a `https://discord.com/channels/` address), and "Take back" where `mayTakeBack` (`POST …/api/stop-reports/<id>/remove`, empty body; 200 `ok`, 403 `NOT_YOUR_REPORT`, 409 `NOTHING_LEFT`, 429).
  - **Reporting:** "Report research here" and "Report Rocket here" in a stop's popup and on a right-click (or long press) on a stop: the PokéStops come from `GET …/api/stops?south&west&north&east` (8M-5M; stops of Pine's own as `pine-<id>`), drawn from a zoom level up or in a "pick a stop" mode as the meetup form does. The research form over `GET …/api/research-options` (`tasks` with `key`, `text`, `category`, `rewards`; `stale`: say a missing task can be typed in full; `rewards` for a typed task's reward, or a Pokémon typed) and up to five stops (the one clicked); `POST …/api/research` `{ task, reward?, stops }` (`task` a list key or text typed in full only: never a view's `typed:<key>`, which is a 422): 200 `{ ok, text, research }`, 404 for a stop gone, 422 with words (`TASK_LIST_CHANGED`: ask the list again), 429. **"Same task at more stops…"** (in a research report's popup and after a report) is further stops picked on the map sent as `POST …/api/research` `{ sameAs: <the report's id>, stops }`: the report's own task, a typed one with its text and reward, a listed one gone from the list since; 409 `REPORT_ENDED` (the report ended or was taken back: report the task anew), counted in `/research report`'s window as the command's menu. **The reward of a typed task is a free text field**, read by the server alone (`readReward`, as `/research`'s `reward:`): a value of `rewards` (`item:<key>`, `energy:<dex>`), `pokemon:<catalogue key>`, or text that names an item of the list, `<species> Mega Energy` of one or a Pokémon in full; anything else is a 422 in its words, `I do not know "…" as a reward. Did you mean A, B or C? Pick one from the list that appears while you type.` (up to five suggestions; the page shows the words as they are, and may offer `rewards` as you type). There is no list of Pokémon for it, none of research-only Pokémon either: any Pokémon the game data knows is taken by its name. The Rocket form over `GET …/api/rocket-options` (`choices` with `key`, `name`, `hideout`, `catches`, Giovanni's `note` shown beside him); `POST …/api/rocket` `{ who, stop, replacing? }`: 200 `{ ok, text, grunt | hideout }`; 409 "Already reported" with the standing `grunt` (open its popup); 422 with `asks.replacing`: **the hideout question as a confirm** ("Replace" sends the same report again with `replacing`; Cancel sends nothing); 503 (posted nowhere: try again); 429.
  - **The filter:** by a reward or a catch (`MapReward.value`: `pokemon:<key>`, `item:<key>`, `energy:<dex>`, the values `/want` and `reward:` use) and by who (`who`: a grunt type, `leader:arlo`, `giovanni`, `decoy`), from the pick lists' names.
- **For staging:** the routes from the browser's console (the page draws none of it yet), as 8X-5's checklist does; a grunt's link and a post's link after a restart (the post's link appears once `start` has drawn it).
- **CLAUDE.md** (Matt or the controller): the status line of 8R-3; the area index line of `web.md` could name reports at stops.

## From Phase 8R-4 (want enhancements, closed 2026-10-22)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-22-phase-8r4-want-enhancements.md`, tasks 39bfb585..2255e937, each equal to its prototype commit; design `2026-10-22-costume-form-wants-design.md` and its mockups, agreed with Matt; the brief read by a fresh Opus reviewer before any code: one Critical (no event page to test against: the controller fetched Leek Duck's Halloween 2026 Part I page and a Spotlight Hour page once, with Matt's go-ahead), seven Important, six Minor, folded in as F1 to F14; the prototype paused for an app restart and moved onto 8X-F (migration 030, per-open tokens on every dialog); prototype review by two Opus reviewers (three Important, sixteen Minor), two fix reviews (five Minor), all folded in; whole-branch review on Opus, high: one Important (a costumed want of every form never fired on its megas: bf841663) and three Minor (92a51d40, 78c998f4, 9b12c133)). Check (9,030 tests), build and database suite (583) green. **Migration 030** (`costumed` on `PokemonNotification` and `TargetWant`); **`GAME_DATA_VERSION` 7** (the first start downloads the game data; a failed download refuses the start); **command changes** (`/want`'s option optional, `/unwant`'s "Team GO Rocket"): deploy the commands before staging. Not yet on Discord: the checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-22-phase-8r4-staging.md`, to run with the 8R-1 to 8R-3 stagings.

- **Matt (2026-10-22), beyond the design:** grunt lineups reported apart are plan **8R-4L** (next; design 4b and S5–S11). A first `/want` outside a post opens with nothing ticked and a box required (no "All" option); in a raid's or max battle's post the Pokémon may be left out and only the post's box is ticked; a boss Pine does not know is refused with a hint. Form names are the catalogue's; `/wants` says "Tier 5 raids". Costumed spawns count only for ordinary events, Community Day, Spotlight Hour, GO Tour, and the Global Wild Area and GO Fest (not the city ones, nothing with UTC times). Nothing ticked takes the want off, also with Remove shown. DMs sent before 8R-4 need no compatibility (only ever on the test server). DMs are not deleted when a report expires.
- **Claude's calls (Matt agreed, 2026-10-22):** megas always plain and out of Costumed (also under a costumed species want, which fires on mega raids as a plain one); Remove leaves the Mega Energy research want; grunt types alphabetical; dialog ids without a token refused; a shadow raid's post ticks Shadow, a shadow egg's Shadow raids.
- **Follow-up (Matt, 2026-10-22), small, after the 8R stagings:** when a spawn or grunt report is taken back or replaced early, edit the DMs it sent to say so ("Taken back by the reporter") and disable their buttons; the DMs' message ids kept in memory for the report's life only (a restart loses them, no worse than today).
- **Follow-up:** staff's view of a member (`src/commands/admin/member-settings.ts`) lists `ctx.wants` with `wantLabel`: a costumed want shows as plain and the `TargetWant` lines (research, Dynamax, Gigantamax, Rocket) are missing (the latter since 8R-1). Reuse `wantEntries` so staff see what `/wants` shows.
- **Follow-up:** Leek Duck's free-text "you might also encounter X wearing …" sentences on event pages are not read, so those rarer costumed spawns are not marked. A global GO Fest or Wild Area whose feed name lacks "Global" is left out.
- **Follow-up candidates:** the `want`/`target` Stop cases of `dm.ts` still read Pokémon and tier ids from before 8R-4 (harmless); other `/wants` code may scan every row per line as `wantEntries` did before its fix.
- **For staging:** the Checkbox component, a required checkbox group, an empty group or dropdown submitted (it must arrive as no values, not an error), and `update` after a dialog opened from an ephemeral list are not yet seen on Discord. A Mega Charizard raid DM to a costumed Charizard want reads "Costumed Charizard · Raids": see whether it reads oddly.

## From Phase 8R-4L (grunt lineups reported apart, closed 2026-10-23)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype (plan `2026-10-23-phase-8r4l-grunt-lineups.md`, tasks 820818f7..69c2cb40, each equal to its prototype commit; design section 4b and S5–S11 of `2026-10-22-costume-form-wants-design.md`).
- **Brief review:** a fresh Opus reviewer read the brief before any code. Three Important findings and eleven Minor, folded in as B1 to B14.
- **Prototype review:** two Opus reviewers. Four Important findings: a report's id no longer telling its version, a refine racing its post, two refines racing, and the first reporter told of their own grunt. Ten Minor.
- **Fix reviews:** two. One Important (versions taken from the clock before the lock) and ten Minor, all folded in.
- **Whole-branch review** on Opus, high: five Minor, fixed with three known items in 9d6cc62a..8debab4f.

Check (9,106 tests), build and database suite (595) green. No migration, no game data change and no command change. Not yet on Discord: the checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-phase-8r4l-staging.md`, to run with the 8R-1 to 8R-4 stagings.

- **Matt (2026-10-22), B2:** a lineup reported over "not sure which" of its type, or over the other lineup, changes the standing report in place. It keeps the same id and the same messages, edited, so DM links keep working. Its version is strictly newer (id and `madeAt`), and an older Take back, `/rocket remove` or the map's take-back answers "changed".
- **Claude's calls:**
  - **The choices:** lineups are named by their first slot, in the feed's order, then "not sure which". With nothing typed, one choice per type; the lineups show once the typed words find a type, while the list fits 25.
  - **When lineups are kept apart:** only for two lineups of the two genders with different, non-empty first slots.
  - **Wants:** they stay by type; a Shadow want is the way to want a lineup's catch.
  - **DMs:** a narrowing changes no DM already sent, so an earlier DM can point at a lineup without the catch it named. The DM edit follow-up of 8R-4 would cover it.
  - **Restarts:** a grunt message's kind is not kept, so after a restart an edit guesses it from the channel's permissions.
- **Follow-up, for the map's UX pass:** the page must send the report's `madeAt` (`MapStopReportBase`) with a take-back. Without it the version is not checked, and a staff take-back can remove a grunt narrowed meanwhile.
- **Follow-up candidate:** `/research remove` takes back by id only. That is right while research reports are never changed in place.
- **For staging:**
  - An older checkout on the shared dev database reads the new-shaped `RocketLineup` rows as unreadable, and leaves the water and untyped grunts out until its next refresh.
  - Narrowing a report needs two accounts reporting at one stop.

## From Phase 8R-5 (research rewards reported apart, closed 2026-10-23)

Built subagent-driven from the reviewed prototype: plan `2026-10-23-phase-8r5-research-rewards.md`, design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-research-rewards-apart-design.md`. The tasks are 46fb6066..2d5954ac, each equal to its prototype commit.

**Matt (2026-10-23), the game facts:**
- A task's "Possible Rewards" on Leek Duck are tasks of their own. A stop has one of them all day, the same for everyone.
- Items and Mega Energy show before the task is done. An encounter shows only after, so with two or more possible encounters a member reports "one of" and narrows it later.

**Matt's rulings:**
- A narrowing to a wanted reward sends a short confirmation DM; nothing is sent for a narrowing to another reward.
- R6: a "one of" narrowed before its DM goes out leaves that stop out of the DM.
- R7: every contradiction asks first, in one dialog of unticked stops; Cancel reports nothing.

**Reviews:**
- **The design:** a fresh Opus reviewer raised R1–R15; thirteen had defaults.
- **The prototype:** two Opus reviewers. Two Important findings: the more-stops menu reporting into a new period, and a typed task's stops listed under another version's line. Sixteen Minor.
- **The fixes:** three rounds and two fix reviews, all Minor; one was a regression in R4's DM kind across regions.
- **Whole-branch review** (Opus, high): one Important finding (a costumed version shown as plain outside the pick list) and four Minor. All were fixed in 1d5b78f1..fca6bf4a, which also made the stored report keep the costume flag.

**Checks:** the check (9,236 tests), the build and the database suite (597) are green. No migration, no game data change and no command option change. Not yet on Discord: the checklist is `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-phase-8r5-staging.md`, to run with the 8R-1 to 8R-4L stagings.

**Claude's calls:**
- **A contradiction of your own report** replaces it without asking.
- **Versions** are worked out lazily, and again when the list or the catalogue changes. A version gone is `TASK_LIST_CHANGED`.
- **The DM's kind** (a confirmation or the usual DM) is decided once per member, across the region channels they view, keeping one DM per report and channel.
- **The checkbox descriptions** give the time as `h:mm a` (`clockText`), since a description cannot show a Discord timestamp.
- **"(costumed)"** is added unless the name already says what the Pokémon wears.
- **Old reports:** reports stored before the switch read as a "one of" (several rewards) or as that reward (one). Those made before the fix wave read as plain.

**Follow-ups:**
- **For the map's UX pass**, the page's part of research versions:
  - the `versions` picker;
  - the `asks` question, with `reports` and `hidden`;
  - "Same task at more stops…": `sameAs` while its report stands, then `task` with `more: true`.
- **Known, accepted:** a menu or map request from before the switch, whose report is a bare key of an items-only task, is refused with "pick the reward" (on the day of the deploy only).

## From the staging run of 8R-1 to 8R-5 (2026-10-23 evening, Googer driven by Claude, Tester by Matt on Android, Googer on iPhone; bot-8r-1.log)

The main checkout at typescript-migration eabb6b2e, normal stage settings, no deploy (the commands deployed at 20:25 for 8X-S are the same set). Before the run: the 58 sponsored stops of `var/sponsored-export/sponsored-stops.csv` seeded on the dev database (31 skipped; a second run adds nothing), and the bot denied Pin Messages and Manage Messages in #squirrelbreeze (its own entry; restore it with allow 17179937808 deny 0). East and West were #north-side and #oakland. Tasks used: "Make 3 Excellent Throws in a row" (Larvitar, Beldum, Gible), "Catch a Ditto" (items only), "Catch 10 Pokémon"; lineups: the water grunts' two.

**Passes:**
- **8R-1:** 3–11, 13, 15 (pre-ticked on Android and iPhone), 16, 17. Steps 7 and 8 ran as Googer by mistake; they show the same.
- **8R-2:** 3, 4, 5, 6 (the message goes at its time; the restart half not run), 7, 9, 10 (the dialog on Android and iPhone), 11, 14, 15.
- **8R-3:** 3, 5 (304 with the ETag, the polls redraw no post), 8–12 from the map page's console; the throttle is shared with Discord.
- **8R-4:** 5–12, 15, 18, 19, as Googer; the per-open token holds (an untick, Cancel, reopen shows the stored boxes).
- **8R-4L:** 2, 3, 9 (the message edited in place, the old Take back answers "changed meanwhile"), 10, 12–16.
- **8R-5:** 2, 3, 6–12, 14, 16–18, 20–23.

**Not run, or could not run:**
- **On the day's tasks:** 8R-5 step 4 (no task with one encounter and items) and step 5 (nothing costumed); 8R-4 steps 20–22 (no costumed raid, reward or spawn).
- **By hand too fast:** 8R-5 step 19 (the DM leaves half a second after the report).
- **Discord's web client:** 8R-4L step 4 (a typed `who:` is replaced by the first suggestion when the field is left).
- **Left for another day:** 8R-1 steps 14, 18–20, 22–24 (rehearsal mode needs other settings); 8R-2 steps 8, 12, 13, 16–18; 8R-3 steps 4, 6, 7; 8R-4 steps 13, 14, 16, 17. The midnight steps ran at 00:00 on 2026-10-24 and pass: 8R-5 step 13 (Googer opened the contradiction dialog at 23:55 and submitted at 00:00:53: "The research period of that report has ended: report the task again with /research report."); 8R-1 step 21 (no pinned post left in the four channels, the StopPost table empty, the summary posts edited at 00:00:01).

**Matt's design feedback (2026-10-23), for mockups before any code:**
- **The pinned post "Today at the stops":** the Pokémon pictures are cut off above and below. A leader's or Giovanni's line shows the Pokémon caught, which is right, but members may expect a picture of the leader instead.
- **Research DMs:** the same cut-off pictures. A "one of" DM shows only the wanted Pokémon's picture while the reward is still open; the game's generic "encounter" picture (from the game's assets) may fit better.
- **Rocket reports:** the message's gallery of every possible catch is too much. One picture: the generic encounter picture when the lineup is not certain, the exact Pokémon when it is, and the leader or Giovanni for theirs.

**Minor findings:**
1. The Rare Candy DMs name the reason "Rare Candy (research)", the Pokémon DMs "Gible · Research".
2. In a channel where the bot may not pin, the answer still says "In the pinned post".
3. A narrowed or changed grunt's answer says "Posted in #north-side" though the message was edited.
4. Once the stop search answered "Loading options failed"; it worked again at once and the bot logged nothing (Discord's side, likely).
5. Typing `rocket` in `/want` lists Rowlet first and "Team GO Rocket" last.
6. Typing "one of" in the task search matches nothing; the "one of" choice is found by its task or Pokémon.
7. "Already reported" counts against the throttle (10 in 30 minutes): Googer met it at the tenth use, with three of them "Already reported". As designed; whether it should count is a question for Matt.
8. The Android dialogs have no Cancel button, only Discord's X (Discord's layout on phones).

**Not staged: a research period that changes mid-day** (Matt, 2026-10-23). An event starting or ending during the day (an intra-day window, a big event's daily hours, a multi-day rotation) is covered by the unit tests of `periodAt` and `nextPeriodChange` only. On Discord it needs made-up data: a copy of ScrapedDuck's `events.json` with an invented event flagged `hasFieldResearchTasks`, starting a few minutes ahead, served on 127.0.0.1 and named in the stage settings' `raidEvents.url`. To watch: the post drawn anew at the start and end, the day's reports waiting during a window and back unchanged after it, reports made inside the window gone after it, a rotation's reports gone for good, and the answers' "until". Note that the task list is read hourly (`research.refreshHours`), so at a real event's start the list may be up to an hour stale. The invented event is kept in the dev database as seen, so delete its row afterwards.

## From Phase 8X-S (max battle screenshots, closed 2026-10-23)

- **Done:** max screenshots by mention, passive reading and `/max screenshot:`; a battle already at the spot (the same boss joins, a newer sure screenshot changes the boss, the rest refused); the moderators told of a Power Spot Pine does not know; passive reading of a clear place in another region for raids too (a party there joined unasked, else Yes / No); party state version 15; "confirmed the end time" / "confirmed the hatch time"; "Power Spot" capitalized. Executed from the prototype rebased onto 8R-5; the whole-branch review (S-1 to S-9) and a review of its fixes (F-1 to F-7) are folded in.

Matt's inputs for it: a Max Battle Day screenshot whose countdown ends at a rotation (step 13), one taken after 21:00 (step 22), one between 05:00 and 06:00 if wanted, and an event announcement picture headed "GIGANTAMAX <species>" in `var/stage/8xs/` (steps 18 and 21).
